## Summary: Camera System Architecture & Third-Person View Implementation Roadmap

### 1. **Camera Setup & Update Per Frame**

**Owner File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/GameRenderer.ts`**

- **Camera Creation (line 52):** `THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, mount.clientWidth / mount.clientHeight, 0.1, 2000)` with 75° FOV
- **Camera Sync (lines 130-136, method `syncCamera`):**
  ```typescript
  private syncCamera(state: GameState): void {
    const { position, yaw, pitch } = state.player;
    this.camera.position.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
    this.camera.rotation.order = "YXZ";
    this.camera.rotation.y = yaw;
    this.camera.rotation.x = pitch;
  }
  ```
  - **Position formula:** Player position + `EYE_HEIGHT` offset (1.62 units, defined in `/lib/game/config.ts:6`)
  - **Rotation order:** YXZ (Euler rotation: yaw first, then pitch)
  - **Updated every frame** in `sync()` method (line 91)

**Camera is added to scene at line 74** (`this.scene.add(this.camera)`), making held item and crack overlay children of the camera.

---

### 2. **Player Body/Visual Representation**

**Critical Finding:** The player has **no visible character body in first-person view**.

- **Held Item (First-Person Hand Model):** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/heldItem.ts`
  - Created as a child of the camera (line 26: `camera.add(root)`)
  - Positioned in camera-local space via `heldItemPose.ts` with base pose at lower-right of FOV (`BASE_POSE: { posX: 0.34, posY: -0.28, posZ: -0.55, ... }`)
  - Animated with swing arcs, walk bob, equip dips, idle sway
  - Model is a 16×16 pixel sprite extruded into 3D voxel mesh for non-block items; simple cube for blocks

- **Crack Overlay (Mining Indicator):** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/crackOverlay.ts`
  - Added to main scene (not camera child, line 149)
  - Positioned at world coordinates of mined block (line 177)
  - 8-stage texture progression based on mining progress

- **Mobs (Other Entities):** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/render/mobVisuals.ts`
  - Created as 3D models in world space (lines 34-35 add to scene)
  - Include bob animation and leg gait, fully visible to observer
  - Player has no equivalent model for themselves

---

### 3. **Mouse-Look / Pointer-Lock Input**

**Owner File: `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/input/inputController.ts`**

**Mouse Movement Handler (lines 46-49):**
```typescript
const onMouseMove = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
  if (!input.pointerLocked) return;
  engine.applyLook(-evt.movementX * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY, -evt.movementY * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY);
};
```
- **Sensitivity:** `MOUSE_SENSITIVITY = 0.0021` (line 4)
- **Calls:** `engine.applyLook(deltaYaw, deltaPitch)` — applies **directly to game state**

**Engine Application (GameEngine.ts, lines 237-241):**
```typescript
applyLook(deltaYaw: number, deltaPitch: number): void {
  const player = this.state.player;
  player.yaw += deltaYaw;
  player.pitch = Math.max(-Math.PI / 2 + 0.01, Math.min(Math.PI / 2 - 0.01, player.pitch + deltaPitch));
}
```
- **Stores:** `state.player.yaw` and `state.player.pitch` (type `PlayerState` in `/lib/game/engine/state.ts:7-14`)
- **Pitch clamped:** ±~90° (prevents gimbal lock)
- **Yaw:** Unbounded, wraps naturally via trig functions

**State Definition (state.ts, lines 10-12):**
```typescript
yaw: number;
pitch: number;
```

---

### 4. **Camera-Relative Systems (All Assume First-Person)**

#### **A. Block Targeting (Mining/Placing) — `/lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts`**
- **Ray Origin (line 13-16):** `eyePosition()` returns `position.y + EYE_HEIGHT`
  ```typescript
  function eyePosition(state: GameState, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
    const { position } = state.player;
    return out.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
  }
  ```
- **Ray Direction (lines 59, 100):** Derived from `lookDirection(yaw, pitch)` 
  ```typescript
  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
  ```
- **Reach:** `MINE_REACH = 7` blocks (config.ts:34)

#### **B. Raycasting Engine (World Queries)** — `/lib/world/queries.ts:11-63`
- DDA voxel traversal algorithm
- **No hardcoded camera assumptions** — operates on origin/direction vectors only
- Returns hit block and "previous" (empty placement cell)

#### **C. Melee Attack / Mob Targeting** — `/lib/game/engine/systems/combat.ts:22-57`
- **Origin (line 24):** `position.y + EYE_HEIGHT`
- **Direction (line 25):** `lookDirection(yaw, pitch)`
- **Reach:** `ATTACK_REACH = 4.5` blocks
- **Aim cone:** Dot product check `ATTACK_AIM_DOT = 0.89` (~~27° half-cone)

#### **D. Audio Panning (3D Spatial Sound)** — `/lib/game/audio/mobAmbience.ts:46-84`
- **Reads:** `playerYaw` directly (line 33)
- **Right vector derivation (lines 56-57):**
  ```typescript
  const rightX = Math.cos(playerYaw);
  const rightZ = -Math.sin(playerYaw);
  ```
- **Panning calculation (line 80):** Dot product of mob-offset with right vector
  - Pans full stereo relative to camera facing
- **No AudioListener object used** — instead, manual stereo panning via `StereoPanner` WebAudio node (synth.ts:74-75)

#### **E. Day-Night Lighting** — `/lib/game/render/GameRenderer.ts:158-168`
- Sun position **world-relative**, not camera-relative (line 162)
- Lighting is **global**, not from camera POV
- No camera-relative directional considerations

#### **F. Fog & Frustum Culling**
- **Fog (line 50):** `THREE.Fog(color, near=30, far=200)`
  - Global to scene, applied uniformly from any camera position
- **Chunk/Region Culling (lines 138-148):**
  - Rebuilds world mesh when player crosses `RENDER_GRID = 20` block boundaries
  - Window size: `±RENDER_RADIUS = 90` blocks from player position
  - **Culling based on player position, not camera position** (but they're identical in first-person)

#### **G. Sky/Horizon Rendering**
- **Background (line 49):** `scene.background = this.liveSky` (solid color, not camera-dependent)
- **No skybox/sky mesh** — plain gradient color interpolated by daylight value (line 166)

---

### 5. **Engine/Renderer Layer Split & Step Order**

**Architecture (docs/architecture.md:1-14):**
```
React UI (components/)
    ↓  useSyncExternalStore + engine commands
GameEngine (lib/game/engine/)        headless, no DOM/rendering
    ↓  reads/writes
Voxel World (lib/world/)             data + queries
    ↑  read every frame by
GameRenderer (lib/game/render/)      Three.js GPU objects
AudioDirector (lib/game/audio/)      WebAudio procedural sound
```

**Per-Frame Step Order (docs/architecture.md:35-47, `GameEngine.step`):**
1. **Stuck detection / auto-unstuck** (STUCK_RESET_SECONDS = 0.8s)
2. **Death check + respawn countdown** (only mobs tick while dead)
3. **Player movement & physics** (`tickPlayerMotion` — derives direction from yaw)
4. **Hunger drain** + health regen
5. **Mining progress & block breaking** (uses raycasting from eye position)
6. **Day-night clock** (`daylightAt()` — single formula)
7. **Hostile mob spawning** (interval/cap)
8. **Mob AI** — wander/aggro/flee with line-of-sight

**Renderer Sync Loop (useMinecraftGame.ts, lines 124-176, the rAF effect):**
```
engine.step(dt, input)
  → engine.consumeEvents() [death/respawn, audio cues]
  → minimap.sync(state) [reads state.worldMeshDirty, clears it]
  → renderer.sync(state) [camera + world mesh + mobs + held item]
  → audio.sync(state, dt) [footsteps, ambient, music]
  → renderer.render() [Three.js WebGL draw]
```

**Critical Constraints:**
- Engine **never imports** renderer (no Three.js in simulation)
- Player state stores only `yaw`/`pitch`; camera is a renderer-only construction
- Raycasting is pure engine logic; renderer doesn't drive targeting

---

### 6. **Critical Touchpoints for Third-Person Camera Implementation**

To implement third-person rear/front views (like Minecraft F5), you must modify/redirect:

| System | Current | Required Change |
|--------|---------|-----------------|
| **Camera Position** | `player.pos + (0, EYE_HEIGHT, 0)` | Offset by camera distance + direction based on view mode |
| **Held Item** | Camera child, camera-space pose | Hide in 3P; need player body model |
| **Raycasting Origin** | `eyePosition()` (eye level) | Keep at eye, OR shift by view offset (depends on UX) |
| **Audio Panning** | `playerYaw` (look direction) | Keep camera yaw, OR track separately |
| **Chunk Culling** | `player.position` (feet) | Keep position-based (camera is offset from same pos) |
| **Crack Overlay** | World space, mine-target | Keep (world-relative, not camera-relative) |
| **Pointer Lock** | Required for gameplay | Same (lock still controls yaw/pitch) |
| **Player Body** | None (FP only) | Create new mesh, parent to scene, sync position/yaw |
| **First-Person Hand** | Camera child | Hide when in 3P mode, show in 1P |

---

### 7. **File Dependencies for Reference**

**Core Camera System:**
- `/lib/game/render/GameRenderer.ts:130-136` — `syncCamera()` is the central camera update
- `/lib/game/engine/state.ts:7-14` — `PlayerState` yaw/pitch storage
- `/lib/game/config.ts:6` — `EYE_HEIGHT = 1.62`
- `/lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts:237-241` — `applyLook()` mutates yaw/pitch

**Raycasting (Eyes Dependent):**
- `/lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts:13-16, 58-60` — mining raycast origin
- `/lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts:97-102` — placement raycast
- `/lib/game/engine/systems/combat.ts:22-26` — attack targeting
- `/lib/world/queries.ts:11-63` — voxel raycast engine (pure, direction-agnostic)

**Audio (Yaw Dependent):**
- `/lib/game/audio/mobAmbience.ts:46-84` — spatial panning from playerYaw
- `/lib/game/audio/synth.ts:74-75` — StereoPanner WebAudio node

**Held Item (Camera Child):**
- `/lib/game/render/heldItem.ts:24-26` — camera.add(root)
- `/lib/game/render/heldItemPose.ts:18, 37-67` — animation pose math

**Input/Control Flow:**
- `/lib/game/input/inputController.ts:46-49` — pointer-lock mousemove handler
- `/lib/game/input/inputController.ts:131-139` — pointer-lock state management

---

### **Summary for Feature Development**

The camera is **tightly coupled** to:
1. **Position:** Via `EYE_HEIGHT` offset in `syncCamera()` — easily offsettable for 3P distance
2. **Rotation:** Via `yaw`/`pitch` in engine state — applies globally to look direction and audio
3. **Raycasting:** Originates from eye, not camera frustum — you can keep mining/attacking from eyes OR shift origin
4. **Held item:** Hard-coded as camera child — must be hidden/swapped for player body in 3P

**Minimal disruption path:** Add a camera mode field to `GameState`, branch `syncCamera()` to apply different offsets/targets based on mode, and conditionally render/hide held-item and player-body meshes. Raycasting can stay eye-relative for familiar gameplay feel.

# Implementation Plan: Camera View Toggle (V key — Minecraft F5 style)

Branch: `feature/camera-view-toggle` off `main`.

## Verified architecture facts (no re-research needed)

- Gameplay is already fully eye-relative: mining (`lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts:15,59,100`), combat (`combat.ts:24-25`), and movement all use `state.player.{yaw,pitch,position}` + `EYE_HEIGHT` via `lookDirection(yaw, pitch, out)` from `lib/game/engine/systems/playerMotion.ts:40-44`. Nothing reads the Three.js camera. Zero gameplay changes needed.
- `lookDirection` convention: forward at yaw=0 is **-Z**; `out.set(-cp*sin(yaw), sin(pitch), -cp*cos(yaw))`. Camera rotation order YXZ with `rotation.y = yaw`, `rotation.x = pitch` (`GameRenderer.ts:130-136`). Positive pitch = looking up.
- `voxelRaycast` (`lib/world/queries.ts:11-63`) has the ray parameter `t` in scope at the hit check but does not return it — it must be added to `RaycastResult` for the camera clamp.
- `toggleDebug` is the session-only-state pattern: `Command` in `commands.ts`, field in `GameState`, dispatch case in `GameEngine.ts:221-225`, mirrored into `GameSnapshot` (`buildSnapshot`, `GameEngine.ts:356-376`), with a matching field in `PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT` (`useMinecraftGame.ts:28-45`). `serialize()` (`GameEngine.ts:244-259`) omits it → session-only for free.
- Input: `KeyV` is free. Keydown handling in `inputController.ts:51-100`; F3 dispatches after the `paused` guard, before `uiBlocked()`.
- Held item is a camera child via a `root` group (`heldItem.ts:24-26`); `HeldItemFrame` is the per-frame param object; `buildModel` (`heldItem.ts:53-68`) is the closure to extract for reuse (block → 0.22 cube; everything else → `buildExtrudedSpriteGeometry(renderSpritePixels(id))`, ~0.48 units wide at default `pixelSize 0.03`).
- Body-model reference pattern: `lib/game/mobModel.ts` (materials/geometries arrays for disposal) + `lib/game/render/mobVisuals.ts` (sync loop, time-phase gait). Note: the mob model builds its face at **+Z** and relies on mob yaw conventions; the player model must build its face at **-Z** so `group.rotation.y = player.yaw` matches the camera convention directly.
- `GameRenderer.triggerSwing()` is called from the rAF loop on the `attackSwung` event (`useMinecraftGame.ts:194`) — fan it out to the body's arm too.
- Pure-pose-module testing style: `heldItemPose.ts` (no Three.js, deterministic in `timeMs`) with colocated `bun test`s; renderer views tested headlessly against a `THREE.Scene` (`mobVisuals.test.ts`).

## Key design decisions

### 1. View mode lives in engine state (recommended)

`GameState.cameraMode` + `toggleCameraView` command, exactly mirroring `debugOpen`/`toggleDebug`. Rationale: the engine is the single dispatch point, the e2e suite asserts through `engine.state`, the snapshot lets PauseMenu/HUD read it later, and `serialize()` omitting it gives session-only persistence with no extra work. Renderer-local state would bypass dispatch and be untestable headlessly.

```ts
// state.ts
export type CameraMode = "first" | "third-rear" | "third-front";
export const CAMERA_MODE_CYCLE: readonly CameraMode[] = ["first", "third-rear", "third-front"];
export function nextCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode;
// GameState: cameraMode: CameraMode;   GameSnapshot: cameraMode: CameraMode;
// commands.ts: | { type: "toggleCameraView" }
```

Dispatch case is unconditional (works while dead/inventory-open, like Minecraft F5; it's render-only). Init value `"first"`.

### 2. Camera math + collision clamp

Constants in `lib/game/config.ts` (Rendering section):

```ts
export const THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE = 4;
export const THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN = 0.2; // > near plane 0.1, avoids clipping into the blocking wall
```

New pure module `lib/game/render/cameraView.ts` (no world access — testable like heldItemPose):

```ts
export type CameraPose = { posX: number; posY: number; posZ: number; yaw: number; pitch: number };
/** Unit direction from the eye toward the desired camera position. Rear: -look. Front: +look. */
export function cameraOffsetDirection(mode: CameraMode, yaw: number, pitch: number, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3;
/** Final pose given an already collision-clamped distance. */
export function computeCameraPose(mode: CameraMode, eyeX: number, eyeY: number, eyeZ: number, yaw: number, pitch: number, distance: number): CameraPose;
```

- **Rear**: position = eye − look·d; rotation `yaw, pitch` (unchanged from first person — camera looks over the player's shoulder along the look direction).
- **Front**: position = eye + look·d; rotation `yaw + Math.PI`, pitch **= −pitch** (camera tilts to face back at the player). This is the answer to design question 4: yes, the *camera's* pitch inverts in front view (player looks up → camera sits up-forward and looks down at the player), but **mouse input is untouched** — `applyLook` still adds to player pitch normally; the on-screen mirroring of horizontal mouse motion in front view is the authentic Minecraft behavior. No input changes.
- **Clamp**: in `GameRenderer.syncCamera`, for third-person modes:
  ```ts
  const eye = (position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
  const dir = cameraOffsetDirection(mode, yaw, pitch, scratch);
  const hit = voxelRaycast(state.world, eye, dir, THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE);
  const distance = hit ? Math.max(0, hit.distance - THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN) : THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE;
  ```
  Requires extending `RaycastResult` in `lib/world/queries.ts` with `distance: number` — the DDA loop's `t` is exactly the entry parameter of the hit cell (return `{ hit, previous, distance: t }`; initial-cell hits give 0 → camera collapses to the eye, a safe degenerate). Renderer importing `voxelRaycast` is architecturally fine (renderer already reads world data).
- Use module-scope scratch `THREE.Vector3`s in the renderer (existing pattern in playerMotion/mining) — no per-frame allocation.
- Fog/far plane/chunk culling need nothing (offset ≤ 4 blocks; culling is keyed to player position).

### 3. Player body model

Three new files under `lib/game/render/`, mirroring the mob split plus the pure-pose convention:

**`playerModel.ts`** — box-geometry humanoid factory, plain `MeshStandardMaterial` colors (Steve-ish palette constants in-file: skin `0xc68e63`, shirt teal, pants indigo, dark shoes, white/dark eye boxes). **Face built on the −Z side.** Limbs use pivot `THREE.Group`s at shoulder/hip with the mesh child offset −length/2 in local Y, so `rotation.x` swings naturally (the mob model rotates meshes about their centers — wrong look for long limbs).

```ts
export type PlayerModel = {
  group: THREE.Group;       // origin at the feet; rotation.y = player.yaw
  head: THREE.Group;        // neck pivot; rotation.x = player.pitch
  leftArm: THREE.Group; rightArm: THREE.Group;   // shoulder pivots
  leftLeg: THREE.Group; rightLeg: THREE.Group;   // hip pivots
  itemHolder: THREE.Group;  // child of rightArm at the hand, slight forward tilt, scale ~0.8
  materials: THREE.Material[];
  geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[];
};
export function createPlayerModel(): PlayerModel;
```

Suggested dimensions (PLAYER_HEIGHT 1.8, EYE_HEIGHT 1.62): legs 0.2×0.72×0.2 at x ±0.11 (hip pivot y 0.72); torso 0.46×0.6×0.24 spanning y 0.72–1.32; arms 0.16×0.66×0.16, shoulder pivots y 1.26 at x ±0.31; head 0.4 cube on a neck pivot at y 1.32 (head center ≈ 1.55, eyes ≈ EYE_HEIGHT). Exact numbers are tunable in the manual pass.

**`playerPose.ts`** — pure, no Three.js:

```ts
export type PlayerPoseInput = { timeMs: number; moveFactor: number; miningActive: boolean; swingStartMs: number };
export type PlayerPose = { leftArmX: number; rightArmX: number; leftLegX: number; rightLegX: number };
export function computePlayerPose(input: PlayerPoseInput): PlayerPose;
```

Walk gait: `sin(timeMs * 0.012) * 0.8 * moveFactor`, legs counter-phased, each arm counter-phased to its same-side leg (stateless time-phase, like mobVisuals). Right-arm swing overlay reusing the heldItemPose timing idiom: one-shot from `swingStartMs` over `SWING_MS`-like 260 ms, looping while `miningActive` — raise/chop `rotationX -= sin(t·π) * ~1.2`.

**`playerVisuals.ts`** — renderer view:

```ts
export type PlayerVisuals = {
  sync(state: GameState, timeMs: number): void;
  triggerSwing(): void;   // latched like heldItem's
  dispose(): void;
};
export function createPlayerVisuals(scene: THREE.Scene): PlayerVisuals;
```

`sync()`: `group.visible = state.cameraMode !== "first" && !state.isDead`; early-return all animation when hidden. `group.position.copy(player.position)` (feet), `group.rotation.y = player.yaw`, `head.rotation.x = player.pitch` (pitch on the head only — body stays upright). `moveFactor` computed exactly as `GameRenderer.sync` does for the held item (line 97). Held item in hand: track `slot.id` key like `heldItem.ts:72-86`; rebuild via the shared builder (below) into `itemHolder`, disposing the old geometry/material on swap.

**Shared item-model builder** — extract `heldItem.ts`'s `buildModel`/`blockColor` (lines 50-68) into a new `lib/game/render/itemModel.ts`:

```ts
export type ItemModel = { object: THREE.Object3D; geometry: THREE.BufferGeometry; material: THREE.Material };
export function buildItemModel(slot: InventorySlot): ItemModel | null;  // null for empty/invalid slots
```

`heldItem.ts` and `playerVisuals.ts` both consume it; each owns disposal of what it built (keeps the existing geometries/materials-array discipline).

**First-person overlay hiding**: add `visible: boolean` to `HeldItemFrame`; `heldItem.update` sets `root.visible = frame.visible` (and skips pose work when hidden). `GameRenderer.sync` passes `visible: state.cameraMode === "first"`.

**`GameRenderer.ts` wiring**: construct `playerVisuals = createPlayerVisuals(this.scene)`; call `this.playerVisuals.sync(state, timeMs)` in `sync()`; `triggerSwing()` fans out to both heldItem and playerVisuals; `dispose()` disposes it; `syncCamera(state)` branches on `state.cameraMode` per section 2.

## Commit slices (imperative subjects, why-bodies per AGENTS.md)

1. **`Add distance to voxelRaycast results`** — `lib/world/queries.ts` (return `{ hit, previous, distance: t }`), `lib/world/queries.test.ts` (e.g. ray from a cell center toward a solid block 5 cells away → distance ≈ 4.5). Body: the third-person camera needs the ray parameter to clamp against walls; existing callers ignore the new field.
2. **`Add camera view mode state with a V-key cycle command`** — `lib/game/engine/state.ts` (`CameraMode`, `nextCameraMode`, `GameState.cameraMode`, `GameSnapshot.cameraMode`), `lib/game/engine/commands.ts` (`toggleCameraView`), `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.ts` (init `"first"`, dispatch case, `buildSnapshot`), `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts` (`PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT.cameraMode: "first"` — required for the type), `lib/game/input/inputController.ts` (`KeyV` → dispatch, placed right after the F3 block), `GameEngine.test.ts` (cycles first → third-rear → third-front → first; default first; `serialize()` output has no camera field; snapshot updates/notifies on toggle). Body: engine is the single dispatch point; session-only like the debug overlay.
3. **`Extract shared held-item model building`** — new `lib/game/render/itemModel.ts`; `heldItem.ts` refactored onto it; existing `heldItem.test.ts` stays green; small `itemModel.test.ts` (block → cube mesh, tool → vertex-colored extruded mesh, empty → null). Body: the third-person hand renders the same item meshes; pure extraction, no behavior change.
4. **`Add humanoid player body model with walk and swing animation`** — new `playerModel.ts`, `playerPose.ts`, `playerVisuals.ts` + tests (model has 6 body parts and disposes cleanly à la mobVisuals tests; pose: gait scales with moveFactor, arms counter-phase legs, mining loops the arm; visuals: hidden in `"first"`, follows position/yaw/pitch, item swaps on slot change). Body: visible player body for third person, following the mob-visuals/box-mesh zero-asset pattern.
5. **`Render third-person camera views with wall clamping`** — new `cameraView.ts` + `cameraView.test.ts` (rear places camera at eye−look·d with passthrough yaw/pitch; front at eye+look·d with yaw+π and −pitch; distance honored); `config.ts` constants; `GameRenderer.ts` (syncCamera branch + voxelRaycast clamp, playerVisuals integration, heldItem `visible` flag, triggerSwing fan-out); `heldItem.ts`/`heldItem.test.ts` for the visible flag; e2e addition in `e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`. Body: camera derives from the same yaw/pitch the gameplay raycasts use, so mining/combat/audio are unchanged by design; clamp raycasts eye→camera so walls never occlude the player.
6. **`Document the V camera view toggle`** — `README.md` Controls (add `V: Camera view` to the line with F3/Esc), `components/game/PauseMenu.tsx` CONTROLS array (`["Camera view", "V"]` — check `PauseMenu.test.tsx` for row assertions), `CHANGELOG.md` new top section (Added: V-key view cycle, humanoid player body, camera wall clamp; "No save-format or worldgen impact"), `docs/testing.md` coverage rows, `docs/architecture.md` if it enumerates GameState fields or render modules (verify; likely yes for both).

Each slice keeps `bun run lint` + `bun test` green for bisectability.

## Test plan

- **Unit (bun test)**: as listed per slice above. The engine test is the spec for cycling and session-only behavior; `cameraView.test.ts` is the spec for the front-view pitch inversion.
- **E2E smoke** (`e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`, slice 5): new test — `calmDaytime`, press `v` → `engine.state.cameraMode === "third-rear"`, `renderedTriangles() > 0` (scene still draws with the body visible), press `v` twice more → `"third-front"` then `"first"`. No pointer lock needed (keydown listeners are document-level). The shared fixture's console-error assertion catches Three.js disposal/NaN regressions.
- **Manual gameplay pass** (required — visual change, per docs/testing.md): cycle V during locked gameplay; body faces the look direction with the face on the correct side; legs/arms swing while walking and stop when idle; right arm chops while mining and on attack clicks; held block/tool visible in the hand and matches the hotbar selection (and disappears on empty slot); first-person overlay hidden in both third-person modes and restored cleanly (no equip-animation glitch); back against a wall and confirm the rear camera clamps without showing through blocks (and front view against a wall ahead); front view: looking up tilts the camera down at you, mouse feel matches Minecraft F5; die in third person (no floating corpse behind the death screen); pause/inventory/F3 all behave normally in each mode.
- **Full baseline before PR**: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`, `bun run test:e2e`.

## Risks / notes

- Worldgen hash tests are untouched (no generation changes); save format untouched (state is session-only and `serialize()` is unmodified).
- The crosshair HUD stays centered in third person — matches Minecraft; call it out in the PR as intentional (hiding it is a possible follow-up).
- Crack overlay is world-space and audio reads player yaw — both verified unaffected.

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { VoxelWorld } from "./voxelWorld";
3	
4	export type RaycastResult = {
5	  hit: THREE.Vector3;
6	  previous: THREE.Vector3;
7	};
8	
9	// DDA voxel traversal. `previous` is the last empty cell before the hit —
10	// face-adjacent to it, never diagonal — which is where placed blocks go.
11	export function voxelRaycast(world: VoxelWorld, origin: THREE.Vector3, direction: THREE.Vector3, maxDist = 6): RaycastResult | null {
12	  const dir = direction.clone().normalize();
13	  const pos = origin.clone();
14	
15	  let x = Math.floor(pos.x);
16	  let y = Math.floor(pos.y);
17	  let z = Math.floor(pos.z);
18	
19	  const stepX = dir.x > 0 ? 1 : -1;
20	  const stepY = dir.y > 0 ? 1 : -1;
21	  const stepZ = dir.z > 0 ? 1 : -1;
22	
23	  const invDx = Math.abs(1 / (dir.x || 1e-6));
24	  const invDy = Math.abs(1 / (dir.y || 1e-6));
25	  const invDz = Math.abs(1 / (dir.z || 1e-6));
26	
27	  let tMaxX = ((stepX > 0 ? x + 1 : x) - pos.x) / (dir.x || 1e-6);
28	  let tMaxY = ((stepY > 0 ? y + 1 : y) - pos.y) / (dir.y || 1e-6);
29	  let tMaxZ = ((stepZ > 0 ? z + 1 : z) - pos.z) / (dir.z || 1e-6);
30	  if (tMaxX < 0) tMaxX += invDx;
31	  if (tMaxY < 0) tMaxY += invDy;
32	  if (tMaxZ < 0) tMaxZ += invDz;
33	
34	  let t = 0;
35	  let previous = new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z);
36	
37	  while (t <= maxDist) {
38	    if (world.isSolid(x, y, z)) return { hit: new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z), previous };
39	    previous = new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z);
40	
41	    if (tMaxX < tMaxY) {
42	      if (tMaxX < tMaxZ) {
43	        x += stepX;
44	        t = tMaxX;
45	        tMaxX += invDx;
46	      } else {
47	        z += stepZ;
48	        t = tMaxZ;
49	        tMaxZ += invDz;
50	      }
51	    } else if (tMaxY < tMaxZ) {
52	      y += stepY;
53	      t = tMaxY;
54	      tMaxY += invDy;
55	    } else {
56	      z += stepZ;
57	      t = tMaxZ;
58	      tMaxZ += invDz;
59	    }
60	  }
61	
62	  return null;
63	}
64	
65	// AABB-vs-voxel test. `position` is the feet center; the box extends halfWidth
66	// on x/z and height on y. Water never collides.
67	export function collidesAt(world: VoxelWorld, position: THREE.Vector3, halfWidth: number, height: number): boolean {
68	  const eps = 0.001;
69	  const minX = Math.floor(position.x - halfWidth + eps);
70	  const maxX = Math.floor(position.x + halfWidth - eps);
71	  const minZ = Math.floor(position.z - halfWidth + eps);
72	  const maxZ = Math.floor(position.z + halfWidth - eps);
73	  const minY = Math.floor(position.y + eps);
74	  const maxY = Math.floor(position.y + height - eps);
75	
76	  for (let y = minY; y <= maxY; y += 1) {
77	    for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z += 1) {
78	      for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += 1) {
79	        if (world.isSolid(x, y, z)) return true;
80	      }
81	    }
82	  }
83	
84	  return false;
85	}
86	
87	export function hasSupportUnderPlayer(world: VoxelWorld, position: THREE.Vector3, halfWidth: number): boolean {
88	  const minX = Math.floor(position.x - halfWidth);
89	  const maxX = Math.floor(position.x + halfWidth);
90	  const minZ = Math.floor(position.z - halfWidth);
91	  const maxZ = Math.floor(position.z + halfWidth);
92	  const y = Math.floor(position.y - 0.05);
93	
94	  for (let z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z += 1) {
95	    for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += 1) {
96	      if (world.isSolid(x, y, z)) return true;
97	    }
98	  }
99	
100	  return false;
101	}
102

94	    this.heldItem.update(state.inventory[state.selectedSlot], {
95	      timeMs,
96	      miningActive: state.mining.targetKey !== "",
97	      moveFactor: state.player.onGround ? Math.min(1, Math.hypot(state.player.velocity.x, state.player.velocity.z) / WALK_SPEED) : 0
98	    });
99	    this.crackOverlay.update(state.mining, state.world);
100	    this.mobVisuals.sync(state.mobs, timeMs);
101	    this.syncDayNight(state);
102	  }
103	
104	  render(): void {
105	    this.webgl.render(this.scene, this.camera);
106	  }
107	
108	  /** One-shot held-item swing for attack clicks (hit or miss). */
109	  triggerSwing(): void {
110	    this.heldItem.triggerSwing();
111	  }
112	
113	  handleResize(): void {
114	    this.camera.aspect = this.mount.clientWidth / this.mount.clientHeight;
115	    this.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
116	    this.webgl.setSize(this.mount.clientWidth, this.mount.clientHeight);
117	  }
118	
119	  dispose(): void {
120	    this.mobVisuals.dispose();
121	    this.crackOverlay.dispose();
122	    this.heldItem.dispose();
123	    this.scene.remove(this.worldMesh);
124	    this.worldMesh.geometry.dispose();
125	    this.worldMaterial.dispose();
126	    this.webgl.dispose();
127	    this.mount.removeChild(this.webgl.domElement);
128	  }
129	
130	  private syncCamera(state: GameState): void {
131	    const { position, yaw, pitch } = state.player;
132	    this.camera.position.set(position.x, position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, position.z);
133	    this.camera.rotation.order = "YXZ";
134	    this.camera.rotation.y = yaw;
135	    this.camera.rotation.x = pitch;
136	  }
137	
138	  private syncWorldMesh(state: GameState): void {
139	    const regionX = Math.floor(state.player.position.x / RENDER_GRID) * RENDER_GRID;
140	    const regionZ = Math.floor(state.player.position.z / RENDER_GRID) * RENDER_GRID;
141	    if (!state.worldMeshDirty && regionX === this.currentRegionX && regionZ === this.currentRegionZ) return;
142	
143	    state.worldMeshDirty = false;
144	    this.currentRegionX = regionX;
145	    this.currentRegionZ = regionZ;
146	
147	    this.rebuildWorldMesh(state.world, regionX, regionZ);
148	  }
149	
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/i-am-thinking-of-composed-platypus.md",
  "content": "# Camera View Toggle: First-Person / Third-Person (Rear & Front)\n\n## Context\n\nAdd a Minecraft-F5-style camera cycle: **V** key switches first-person → third-person rear (camera behind the player) → third-person front (camera facing the player) → back to first. Today the game is first-person only — the camera is rebuilt each frame from `player.yaw/pitch` + `EYE_HEIGHT`, and there is no player body mesh (only a held-item overlay parented to the camera).\n\nThe architecture makes this a presentation-layer feature: mining, placing, combat, movement, and audio panning all read player yaw/pitch/eye position from engine state, never the Three.js camera, so gameplay stays eye-relative and untouched in every mode. The two real work items are a humanoid player body (visible only in third person) and camera wall-clamping so the third-person camera never sees through blocks.\n\nDecisions already made with the user: **V** key (not F5 — browser refresh conflict), **proper humanoid model** (head, torso, 2 arms, 2 legs, walk-swing animation, held item shown in hand). View mode is session-only (not saved), like the debug overlay.\n\nBranch: `feature/camera-view-toggle` off `main`.\n\n## Design\n\n### View mode in engine state (mirrors `toggleDebug`)\n\n- `lib/game/engine/state.ts`: `export type CameraMode = \"first\" | \"third-rear\" | \"third-front\"`, a `nextCameraMode()` cycle helper, `GameState.cameraMode` + `GameSnapshot.cameraMode` (init `\"first\"`).\n- `lib/game/engine/commands.ts`: `{ type: \"toggleCameraView\" }`; unconditional dispatch case in `GameEngine.ts` (works while dead/paused — render-only, like Minecraft).\n- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`: add `cameraMode: \"first\"` to `PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT`.\n- `lib/game/input/inputController.ts`: `KeyV` → dispatch, placed next to the F3 handler.\n- `serialize()` is untouched → session-only persistence for free. No save-format impact.\n\n### Camera math (`lib/game/render/cameraView.ts`, new, pure — testable like `heldItemPose.ts`)\n\nConventions (verified): forward at yaw=0 is **−Z** (`lookDirection` in playerMotion.ts), camera rotation order YXZ, positive pitch = up.\n\n- **Rear**: camera = eye − look·d; rotation `(yaw, pitch)` unchanged.\n- **Front**: camera = eye + look·d; rotation `(yaw + π, −pitch)` — the camera faces back at the player; mouse input is untouched (`applyLook` unchanged), so horizontal motion appears mirrored on screen, which is authentic Minecraft F5 behavior.\n- Exports: `cameraOffsetDirection(mode, yaw, pitch, out)` and `computeCameraPose(mode, eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ, yaw, pitch, distance)`.\n\n**Wall clamp** in `GameRenderer.syncCamera` for third-person modes: raycast eye → offset direction with the existing `voxelRaycast` (`lib/world/queries.ts:11`), clamp `distance = hit ? max(0, hit.distance − THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN) : THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE`. Requires extending `RaycastResult` with `distance: number` — the DDA loop variable `t` at `queries.ts:38` is exactly the hit-cell entry parameter; return it. Renderer already reads world data, so importing `voxelRaycast` there is architecturally fine. Use module-scope scratch `Vector3`s (existing pattern).\n\nConstants in `lib/game/config.ts`: `THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE = 4`, `THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN = 0.2` (> near plane 0.1).\n\nFog, chunk culling (player-position-keyed), crack overlay (world-space), and audio (player-yaw-based) all verified unaffected.\n\n### Player body (new files under `lib/game/render/`, mirroring the mob-visuals split)\n\n- **`playerModel.ts`** — box-geometry humanoid factory, vertex-color materials, zero binary assets (Steve-ish palette: skin/shirt/pants constants in-file). **Face built on the −Z side** so `group.rotation.y = player.yaw` matches the camera convention (the mob model faces +Z — don't copy blindly). Limbs are pivot `Group`s at shoulder/hip with the mesh offset −length/2, so `rotation.x` swings naturally. Returns `{ group, head, leftArm, rightArm, leftLeg, rightLeg, itemHolder, materials, geometries }`. Suggested dims (PLAYER_HEIGHT 1.8, eye ≈ 1.62): legs 0.2×0.72×0.2, torso 0.46×0.6×0.24 (y 0.72–1.32), arms 0.16×0.66×0.16 at shoulders y 1.26, head 0.4 cube on neck pivot y 1.32; tune in the manual pass.\n- **`playerPose.ts`** — pure, no Three.js: `computePlayerPose({ timeMs, moveFactor, miningActive, swingStartMs })` → limb `rotation.x` values. Walk gait `sin(timeMs·0.012)·0.8·moveFactor`, legs counter-phased, arms counter-phased to same-side legs; right-arm chop overlay reusing the `heldItemPose.ts` swing-timing idiom (one-shot ~260 ms, looping while mining).\n- **`playerVisuals.ts`** — renderer view `{ sync(state, timeMs), triggerSwing(), dispose() }`. `sync`: `group.visible = state.cameraMode !== \"first\" && !state.isDead`; position at feet, `rotation.y = yaw`, pitch on the **head only**; `moveFactor` computed exactly as `GameRenderer.sync` does for the held item (GameRenderer.ts:97); held item rebuilt into `itemHolder` on `slot.id` change (track-and-dispose like `heldItem.ts:72-86`).\n- **`itemModel.ts`** (new) — extract `heldItem.ts`'s `buildModel`/`blockColor` (lines ~50-68: block → cube, else extruded sprite) into a shared `buildItemModel(slot)`; consumed by both `heldItem.ts` and `playerVisuals.ts`, each disposing what it built.\n\n### First-person overlay hiding\n\nAdd `visible: boolean` to `HeldItemFrame`; `heldItem.update` sets `root.visible` and skips pose work when hidden. `GameRenderer.sync` passes `visible: state.cameraMode === \"first\"`. `GameRenderer.triggerSwing()` fans out to both heldItem and playerVisuals.\n\n## Commit slices (each keeps lint + tests green)\n\n1. **Add distance to voxelRaycast results** — `lib/world/queries.ts` + test (ray toward a block N cells away → expected distance). Why-body: third-person camera needs the ray parameter to clamp against walls; existing callers ignore the field.\n2. **Add camera view mode state with a V-key cycle command** — state.ts, commands.ts, GameEngine.ts, useMinecraftGame.ts, inputController.ts + GameEngine tests (cycle order, default `\"first\"`, `serialize()` output has no camera field, snapshot notifies).\n3. **Extract shared held-item model building** — `itemModel.ts`, refactor `heldItem.ts` onto it; existing heldItem tests stay green + small itemModel test. Pure extraction, no behavior change.\n4. **Add humanoid player body model with walk and swing animation** — `playerModel.ts`, `playerPose.ts`, `playerVisuals.ts` + tests (parts/disposal à la mobVisuals tests; gait scales with moveFactor; hidden in `\"first\"`; item swaps on slot change).\n5. **Render third-person camera views with wall clamping** — `cameraView.ts` + test (rear passthrough yaw/pitch; front yaw+π / −pitch), config constants, `GameRenderer.ts` wiring (syncCamera branch, clamp, playerVisuals, heldItem visible flag, swing fan-out), e2e smoke addition.\n6. **Document the V camera view toggle** — README controls, `components/game/PauseMenu.tsx` CONTROLS array (~line 14-24, check PauseMenu tests), CHANGELOG entry (\"no save-format or worldgen impact\"), docs/testing.md coverage row, docs/architecture.md if it lists GameState fields/render modules.\n\n## Verification\n\n- **Unit**: per-slice tests above; engine test is the spec for cycling/session-only; cameraView test is the spec for front-view inversion.\n- **E2E** (`e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`): press `v` → `engine.state.cameraMode === \"third-rear\"` and `renderedTriangles() > 0`; two more presses → `\"third-front\"` then `\"first\"`. Document-level keydown — no pointer lock needed.\n- **Manual gameplay pass** (required — visual + pointer-lock-adjacent, per docs/testing.md): body faces look direction; limbs swing while walking, idle when still; right arm chops on mining/attack; held item in hand matches hotbar; overlay hidden in third person and restored cleanly; rear camera clamps against a wall behind (front view: wall ahead); front view tilt feels like Minecraft F5; dying in third person doesn't leave a floating body; pause/inventory/F3 work in all modes; crosshair stays centered (intentional, matches Minecraft — note in PR).\n- **Baseline before PR**: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`, `bun run test:e2e`.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Camera View Toggle: First-Person / Third-Person (Rear & Front)

## Context

Add a Minecraft-F5-style camera cycle: **V** key switches first-person → third-person rear (camera behind the player) → third-person front (camera facing the player) → back to first. Today the game is first-person only — the camera is rebuilt each frame from `player.yaw/pitch` + `EYE_HEIGHT`, and there is no player body mesh (only a held-item overlay parented to the camera).

The architecture makes this a presentation-layer feature: mining, placing, combat, movement, and audio panning all read player yaw/pitch/eye position from engine state, never the Three.js camera, so gameplay stays eye-relative and untouched in every mode. The two real work items are a humanoid player body (visible only in third person) and camera wall-clamping so the third-person camera never sees through blocks.

Decisions already made with the user: **V** key (not F5 — browser refresh conflict), **proper humanoid model** (head, torso, 2 arms, 2 legs, walk-swing animation, held item shown in hand). View mode is session-only (not saved), like the debug overlay.

Branch: `feature/camera-view-toggle` off `main`.

## Design

### View mode in engine state (mirrors `toggleDebug`)

- `lib/game/engine/state.ts`: `export type CameraMode = "first" | "third-rear" | "third-front"`, a `nextCameraMode()` cycle helper, `GameState.cameraMode` + `GameSnapshot.cameraMode` (init `"first"`).
- `lib/game/engine/commands.ts`: `{ type: "toggleCameraView" }`; unconditional dispatch case in `GameEngine.ts` (works while dead/paused — render-only, like Minecraft).
- `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`: add `cameraMode: "first"` to `PRE_MOUNT_SNAPSHOT`.
- `lib/game/input/inputController.ts`: `KeyV` → dispatch, placed next to the F3 handler.
- `serialize()` is untouched → session-only persistence for free. No save-format impact.

### Camera math (`lib/game/render/cameraView.ts`, new, pure — testable like `heldItemPose.ts`)

Conventions (verified): forward at yaw=0 is **−Z** (`lookDirection` in playerMotion.ts), camera rotation order YXZ, positive pitch = up.

- **Rear**: camera = eye − look·d; rotation `(yaw, pitch)` unchanged.
- **Front**: camera = eye + look·d; rotation `(yaw + π, −pitch)` — the camera faces back at the player; mouse input is untouched (`applyLook` unchanged), so horizontal motion appears mirrored on screen, which is authentic Minecraft F5 behavior.
- Exports: `cameraOffsetDirection(mode, yaw, pitch, out)` and `computeCameraPose(mode, eyeX, eyeY, eyeZ, yaw, pitch, distance)`.

**Wall clamp** in `GameRenderer.syncCamera` for third-person modes: raycast eye → offset direction with the existing `voxelRaycast` (`lib/world/queries.ts:11`), clamp `distance = hit ? max(0, hit.distance − THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN) : THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE`. Requires extending `RaycastResult` with `distance: number` — the DDA loop variable `t` at `queries.ts:38` is exactly the hit-cell entry parameter; return it. Renderer already reads world data, so importing `voxelRaycast` there is architecturally fine. Use module-scope scratch `Vector3`s (existing pattern).

Constants in `lib/game/config.ts`: `THIRD_PERSON_DISTANCE = 4`, `THIRD_PERSON_MARGIN = 0.2` (> near plane 0.1).

Fog, chunk culling (player-position-keyed), crack overlay (world-space), and audio (player-yaw-based) all verified unaffected.

### Player body (new files under `lib/game/render/`, mirroring the mob-visuals split)

- **`playerModel.ts`** — box-geometry humanoid factory, vertex-color materials, zero binary assets (Steve-ish palette: skin/shirt/pants constants in-file). **Face built on the −Z side** so `group.rotation.y = player.yaw` matches the camera convention (the mob model faces +Z — don't copy blindly). Limbs are pivot `Group`s at shoulder/hip with the mesh offset −length/2, so `rotation.x` swings naturally. Returns `{ group, head, leftArm, rightArm, leftLeg, rightLeg, itemHolder, materials, geometries }`. Suggested dims (PLAYER_HEIGHT 1.8, eye ≈ 1.62): legs 0.2×0.72×0.2, torso 0.46×0.6×0.24 (y 0.72–1.32), arms 0.16×0.66×0.16 at shoulders y 1.26, head 0.4 cube on neck pivot y 1.32; tune in the manual pass.
- **`playerPose.ts`** — pure, no Three.js: `computePlayerPose({ timeMs, moveFactor, miningActive, swingStartMs })` → limb `rotation.x` values. Walk gait `sin(timeMs·0.012)·0.8·moveFactor`, legs counter-phased, arms counter-phased to same-side legs; right-arm chop overlay reusing the `heldItemPose.ts` swing-timing idiom (one-shot ~260 ms, looping while mining).
- **`playerVisuals.ts`** — renderer view `{ sync(state, timeMs), triggerSwing(), dispose() }`. `sync`: `group.visible = state.cameraMode !== "first" && !state.isDead`; position at feet, `rotation.y = yaw`, pitch on the **head only**; `moveFactor` computed exactly as `GameRenderer.sync` does for the held item (GameRenderer.ts:97); held item rebuilt into `itemHolder` on `slot.id` change (track-and-dispose like `heldItem.ts:72-86`).
- **`itemModel.ts`** (new) — extract `heldItem.ts`'s `buildModel`/`blockColor` (lines ~50-68: block → cube, else extruded sprite) into a shared `buildItemModel(slot)`; consumed by both `heldItem.ts` and `playerVisuals.ts`, each disposing what it built.

### First-person overlay hiding

Add `visible: boolean` to `HeldItemFrame`; `heldItem.update` sets `root.visible` and skips pose work when hidden. `GameRenderer.sync` passes `visible: state.cameraMode === "first"`. `GameRenderer.triggerSwing()` fans out to both heldItem and playerVisuals.

## Commit slices (each keeps lint + tests green)

1. **Add distance to voxelRaycast results** — `lib/world/queries.ts` + test (ray toward a block N cells away → expected distance). Why-body: third-person camera needs the ray parameter to clamp against walls; existing callers ignore the field.
2. **Add camera view mode state with a V-key cycle command** — state.ts, commands.ts, GameEngine.ts, useMinecraftGame.ts, inputController.ts + GameEngine tests (cycle order, default `"first"`, `serialize()` output has no camera field, snapshot notifies).
3. **Extract shared held-item model building** — `itemModel.ts`, refactor `heldItem.ts` onto it; existing heldItem tests stay green + small itemModel test. Pure extraction, no behavior change.
4. **Add humanoid player body model with walk and swing animation** — `playerModel.ts`, `playerPose.ts`, `playerVisuals.ts` + tests (parts/disposal à la mobVisuals tests; gait scales with moveFactor; hidden in `"first"`; item swaps on slot change).
5. **Render third-person camera views with wall clamping** — `cameraView.ts` + test (rear passthrough yaw/pitch; front yaw+π / −pitch), config constants, `GameRenderer.ts` wiring (syncCamera branch, clamp, playerVisuals, heldItem visible flag, swing fan-out), e2e smoke addition.
6. **Document the V camera view toggle** — README controls, `components/game/PauseMenu.tsx` CONTROLS array (~line 14-24, check PauseMenu tests), CHANGELOG entry ("no save-format or worldgen impact"), docs/testing.md coverage row, docs/architecture.md if it lists GameState fields/render modules.

## Verification

- **Unit**: per-slice tests above; engine test is the spec for cycling/session-only; cameraView test is the spec for front-view inversion.
- **E2E** (`e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`): press `v` → `engine.state.cameraMode === "third-rear"` and `renderedTriangles() > 0`; two more presses → `"third-front"` then `"first"`. Document-level keydown — no pointer lock needed.
- **Manual gameplay pass** (required — visual + pointer-lock-adjacent, per docs/testing.md): body faces look direction; limbs swing while walking, idle when still; right arm chops on mining/attack; held item in hand matches hotbar; overlay hidden in third person and restored cleanly; rear camera clamps against a wall behind (front view: wall ahead); front view tilt feels like Minecraft F5; dying in third person doesn't leave a floating body; pause/inventory/F3 work in all modes; crosshair stays centered (intentional, matches Minecraft — note in PR).
- **Baseline before PR**: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`, `bun run test:e2e`.

## Branch & PR (user-authorized)

All work on `feature/camera-view-toggle` off `main`, committed in the six slices above. Once the full verification baseline is green, push the branch and open a PR against this fork's `main` (`gh pr create --repo hutusi/monecraft --base main`), no AI-attribution lines in the description. The PR description will note: gameplay raycasts/audio intentionally unchanged (eye-relative in all modes), crosshair stays centered in third person (matches Minecraft), no save-format or worldgen impact, and that the manual gameplay pass items above should be checked in-browser before merge.

1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import * as THREE from "three";
3	import { BlockId, VoxelWorld, collidesAt, hasSupportUnderPlayer, voxelRaycast } from "@/lib/world";
4	import { createSurfaceYAt } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
5	
6	function emptyWorld(): VoxelWorld {
7	  return new VoxelWorld(16, 16, 16, 1);
8	}
9	
10	describe("voxelRaycast", () => {
11	  test("axis-aligned ray hits the first solid block and reports the cell before it", () => {
12	    const world = emptyWorld();
13	    world.set(8, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
14	    const result = voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(2.5, 5.5, 5.5), new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), 10);
15	    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
16	    expect(result!.hit.toArray()).toEqual([8, 5, 5]);
17	    expect(result!.previous.toArray()).toEqual([7, 5, 5]);
18	  });
19	
20	  test("diagonal ray traverses cells without skipping through corners", () => {
21	    const world = emptyWorld();
22	    world.set(6, 6, 6, BlockId.Stone);
23	    const result = voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(2.5, 2.5, 2.5), new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1), 12);
24	    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
25	    expect(result!.hit.toArray()).toEqual([6, 6, 6]);
26	    // The previous cell must be face-adjacent to the hit, never diagonal.
27	    const diff = result!.hit.clone().sub(result!.previous);
28	    expect(Math.abs(diff.x) + Math.abs(diff.y) + Math.abs(diff.z)).toBe(1);
29	  });
30	
31	  test("returns null when no solid block is within maxDist", () => {
32	    const world = emptyWorld();
33	    world.set(12, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
34	    expect(voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(2.5, 5.5, 5.5), new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), 5)).toBeNull();
35	  });
36	
37	  test("ray starting inside a solid block hits it immediately", () => {
38	    const world = emptyWorld();
39	    world.set(5, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
40	    const result = voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 5.5, 5.5), new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), 5);
41	    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
42	    expect(result!.hit.toArray()).toEqual([5, 5, 5]);
43	  });
44	
45	  test("water is not a raycast target", () => {
46	    const world = emptyWorld();
47	    world.set(8, 5, 5, BlockId.Water);
48	    expect(voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(2.5, 5.5, 5.5), new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), 10)).toBeNull();
49	  });
50	
51	  test("near-zero direction components do not divide by zero", () => {
52	    const world = emptyWorld();
53	    world.set(5, 8, 5, BlockId.Stone);
54	    const result = voxelRaycast(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 2.5, 5.5), new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), 10);
55	    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
56	    expect(result!.hit.toArray()).toEqual([5, 8, 5]);
57	  });
58	});
59	
60	describe("collidesAt", () => {
61	  // Player AABB: position is the feet center, halfWidth extends on x/z, height on y.
62	  test("collides when feet overlap a solid block", () => {
63	    const world = emptyWorld();
64	    world.set(5, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
65	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 5.5, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(true);
66	  });
67	
68	  test("does not collide when standing on top of a block", () => {
69	    const world = emptyWorld();
70	    world.set(5, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
71	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 6.0, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(false);
72	  });
73	
74	  test("collides when the head overlaps a block above", () => {
75	    const world = emptyWorld();
76	    world.set(5, 7, 5, BlockId.Stone);
77	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 6.0, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(true);
78	  });
79	
80	  test("water is not a collision", () => {
81	    const world = emptyWorld();
82	    world.set(5, 5, 5, BlockId.Water);
83	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 5.5, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(false);
84	  });
85	
86	  test("wide hitboxes clip blocks adjacent to the center column", () => {
87	    const world = emptyWorld();
88	    world.set(6, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
89	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.9, 5.5, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(true);
90	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.5, 5.5, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(false);
91	  });
92	
93	  test("epsilon keeps a position exactly on a block boundary from colliding with the far cell", () => {
94	    const world = emptyWorld();
95	    world.set(6, 5, 5, BlockId.Stone);
96	    // Right edge of the AABB lands exactly on x=6.0; the epsilon excludes cell 6.
97	    expect(collidesAt(world, new THREE.Vector3(5.7, 5.5, 5.5), 0.3, 1.8)).toBe(false);
98	  });
99	});

101	describe("hasSupportUnderPlayer", () => {
102	  test("detects solid ground directly below the feet", () => {
103	    const world = emptyWorld();

106	  });

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { collidesAt, generateWorld, VoxelWorld, WORLD_SIZE_X, WORLD_SIZE_Y, WORLD_SIZE_Z } from "@/lib/world";
3	import { HOTBAR_SLOTS, MAX_HUNGER, MAX_HEARTS, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT, RENDER_RADIUS, STUCK_RESET_SECONDS } from "@/lib/game/config";
4	import { createEmptyArmorEquipment, createInitialInventory } from "@/lib/game/items";
5	import { RECIPES } from "@/lib/game/recipes";
6	import * as inv from "@/lib/game/inventory";
7	import { inventorySlotsSnapshot, restoreEquippedArmor, restoreInventorySlots, restoreSelectedSlot } from "@/lib/game/save";
8	import { createSurfaceYAt, findSpawnOnLand, randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
9	import type { SaveData } from "@/lib/game/types";
10	import { createBlockChangeTracker } from "./blockChanges";
11	import type { Command } from "./commands";
12	import { createTimers, type FrameInput, type GameEvent, type GameSnapshot, type GameState } from "./state";
13	import { daylightAt, tickDayNight } from "./systems/dayNight";
14	import { applyDamageWithArmor, tickRespawnTimer } from "./systems/playerLife";
15	import { tickPlayerMotion } from "./systems/playerMotion";
16	import { restoreHunger, tickHungerDrain, tickHealthRegen } from "./systems/playerStats";
17	import { placeSelectedBlock, resetMining, tickMining } from "./systems/mining";
18	import { tryAttackMob, weaponDamage } from "./systems/combat";
19	import { tickMobs } from "./systems/mobAI";
20	import { spawnInitialMobs, tickHostileSpawnDirector } from "./systems/spawnDirector";
21	
22	export type GameEngineOptions = {
23	  /** A parsed save to restore, or null for a fresh world. */
24	  save?: SaveData | null;
25	  /** Seed for a fresh world; ignored when a save is provided. */
26	  seed?: number;
27	  /** Randomness source for mob spawning/AI — injectable for deterministic tests. */
28	  rng?: () => number;
29	  /** World dimensions override for fast headless tests. */
30	  worldSize?: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
31	};
32	
33	/**
34	 * The framework-agnostic game core. Owns all simulation state, advances it in
35	 * step(), and accepts player intents through dispatch(). No React, no DOM, no
36	 * rendering — the renderer reads state, the React shell subscribes to
37	 * snapshots via subscribe()/getSnapshot() (useSyncExternalStore compatible).
38	 */
39	export class GameEngine {
40	  readonly state: GameState;
41	  private readonly rng: () => number;
42	  private readonly surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
43	  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
44	  private events: GameEvent[] = [];
45	  private snapshot: GameSnapshot;
46	
47	  constructor(options: GameEngineOptions = {}) {
48	    const save = options.save ?? null;
49	    this.rng = options.rng ?? Math.random;
50	
51	    const seed = save?.seed ?? options.seed ?? Math.floor(Math.random() * 2147483647);
52	    const size = options.worldSize ?? { x: WORLD_SIZE_X, y: WORLD_SIZE_Y, z: WORLD_SIZE_Z };
53	    const world = new VoxelWorld(size.x, size.y, size.z, seed);
54	    generateWorld(world);
55	
56	    const blockChanges = createBlockChangeTracker(world);
57	    if (save) blockChanges.applySavedChanges(save.changes);
58	
59	    this.surfaceYAt = createSurfaceYAt(world);
60	
61	    const firstSpawn = findSpawnOnLand(world, Math.floor(world.sizeX / 2), Math.floor(world.sizeZ / 2));
62	    this.state = {
63	      world,
64	      blockChanges,
65	      player: {
66	        position: new THREE.Vector3(firstSpawn.x, firstSpawn.y, firstSpawn.z),
67	        velocity: new THREE.Vector3(),
68	        yaw: 0,
69	        pitch: 0,
70	        onGround: false
71	      },
72	      inventory: createInitialInventory(),
73	      equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
74	      selectedSlot: 0,
75	      hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
76	      hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
77	      isDead: false,
78	      respawnTimer: 0,
79	      inventoryOpen: false,
80	      paused: false,
81	      debugOpen: false,
82	      debugInfo: null,
83	      capsActive: false,
84	      mobs: [],
85	      nextMobId: 1,
86	      dayClock: 0,
87	      daylight: daylightAt(0),
88	      daylightPercent: Math.round(daylightAt(0) * 100),
89	      mining: { targetKey: "", progress: 0 },
90	      timers: createTimers(),
91	      worldMeshDirty: true
92	    };
93	
94	    if (save) {
95	      this.state.inventory = restoreInventorySlots(save) ?? this.state.inventory;
96	      this.state.equippedArmor = restoreEquippedArmor(save) ?? this.state.equippedArmor;
97	      this.state.selectedSlot = restoreSelectedSlot(save) ?? this.state.selectedSlot;
98	      if (save.player) this.state.player.position.set(save.player.x, save.player.y, save.player.z);
99	    }
100	
101	    // Safety check: if stuck after load, relocate to a plain.
102	    if (collidesAt(world, this.state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || this.state.player.position.y < 2) {
103	      this.forceUnstuck();
104	    }
105	
106	    spawnInitialMobs(this.state, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
107	    this.snapshot = this.buildSnapshot();
108	  }
109	
110	  /** Advances the simulation by dt seconds. The renderer draws the state afterwards. */
111	  step(dt: number, input: FrameInput): void {
112	    const state = this.state;
113	    if (state.paused) {
114	      // Full freeze: mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all stop.
115	      this.refreshSnapshot();
116	      return;
117	    }
118	    state.capsActive = input.capsActive;
119	
120	    // Stuck detection / auto-unstuck.
121	    const inBadState = collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2;
122	    state.timers.stuckTimer = inBadState ? state.timers.stuckTimer + dt : 0;
123	    if (state.timers.stuckTimer > STUCK_RESET_SECONDS) {
124	      this.forceUnstuck();
125	      state.timers.stuckTimer = 0;
126	    }
127	
128	    // Death: only mobs and the respawn countdown tick while dead.
129	    if (state.isDead) {
130	      if (tickRespawnTimer(state, dt)) this.respawn();
131	      else tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
132	      this.refreshSnapshot();
133	      return;
134	    }
135	
136	    const move = tickPlayerMotion(state, input, dt, this.applyDamage);
137	    if (move.didJump) this.emit({ type: "jumped" });
138	    if (move.didLand) this.emit({ type: "landed", impact: move.landImpact });
139	    tickHungerDrain(state, move);
140	    tickHealthRegen(state, dt);
141	    tickMining(state, input, dt, this.emit);
142	    tickDayNight(state, dt);
143	    tickHostileSpawnDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
144	    tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
145	    this.tickDebugInfo(dt);
146	
147	    this.refreshSnapshot();
148	  }
149	
150	  /** Applies a discrete player intent. */
151	  dispatch(command: Command): void {
152	    const state = this.state;
153	    switch (command.type) {
154	      case "selectSlot": {
155	        if (command.index >= 0 && command.index < Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS, state.inventory.length)) {
156	          state.selectedSlot = command.index;
157	        }
158	        break;
159	      }
160	      case "toggleInventory": {
161	        state.inventoryOpen = !state.inventoryOpen;
162	        break;
163	      }
164	      case "craft": {
165	        const recipe = RECIPES.find((entry) => entry.id === command.recipeId);
166	        if (!recipe || state.isDead) break;
167	        state.inventory = inv.craft(state.inventory, recipe) ?? state.inventory;
168	        break;
169	      }
170	      case "swapSlots": {
171	        state.inventory = inv.swapSlots(state.inventory, command.from, command.to) ?? state.inventory;
172	        break;
173	      }
174	      case "toggleEquipArmor": {
175	        state.equippedArmor = inv.toggleEquipArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor, command.index) ?? state.equippedArmor;
176	        break;
177	      }
178	      case "eatFood": {
179	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen) break;
180	        const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
181	        if (!slot?.id || slot.id !== "food" || slot.count <= 0) break;
182	        const next = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, "food", -1, state.selectedSlot);
183	        if (!next) break;
184	        state.inventory = next;
185	        state.hunger = restoreHunger(state.hunger);
186	        this.emit({ type: "ateFood" });
187	        break;
188	      }
189	      case "placeBlock": {
190	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen) break;
191	        placeSelectedBlock(state, this.emit);
192	        break;
193	      }
194	      case "attack": {
195	        if (state.isDead || state.inventoryOpen) break;
196	        this.emit({ type: "attackSwung" });
197	        const hitKind = tryAttackMob(state, weaponDamage(state), this.removeMobAt);
198	        if (hitKind) {
199	          this.emit({ type: "mobHit", kind: hitKind });
200	          state.inventory = inv.consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
201	          resetMining(state);
202	        }
203	        break;
204	      }
205	      case "unstuck": {
206	        if (state.isDead) break;
207	        this.forceUnstuck();
208	        break;
209	      }
210	      case "pause": {
211	        // The inventory panel and the death screen own their lock-loss; only
212	        // plain gameplay lock-loss (or an explicit Escape) opens the pause menu.
213	        if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead) break;
214	        state.paused = true;
215	        break;
216	      }
217	      case "resume": {
218	        state.paused = false;
219	        break;
220	      }
221	      case "toggleDebug": {
222	        state.debugOpen = !state.debugOpen;
223	        state.debugInfo = state.debugOpen ? this.currentDebugInfo() : null;
224	        break;
225	      }
226	      case "respawn": {
227	        // Skip the rest of the countdown; the next step performs the respawn.
228	        if (state.isDead) state.respawnTimer = 0;
229	        break;
230	      }
231	    }
232	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
233	    this.refreshSnapshot();
234	  }
235	
236	  /** Mouse-look: applied directly by the input controller (radians). */
237	  applyLook(deltaYaw: number, deltaPitch: number): void {
238	    const player = this.state.player;
239	    player.yaw += deltaYaw;
240	    player.pitch = Math.max(-Math.PI / 2 + 0.01, Math.min(Math.PI / 2 - 0.01, player.pitch + deltaPitch));
241	  }
242	
243	  /** Current world + player state as a persistable save. */
244	  serialize(): SaveData {
245	    const state = this.state;
246	    return {
247	      version: 2,
248	      seed: state.world.seed,
249	      changes: state.blockChanges.changes(),
250	      inventorySlots: inventorySlotsSnapshot(state.inventory),
251	      equippedArmor: { ...state.equippedArmor },
252	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
253	      player: {
254	        x: state.player.position.x,
255	        y: state.player.position.y,
256	        z: state.player.position.z
257	      }
258	    };
259	  }
260	
261	  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
262	    this.listeners.add(listener);
263	    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
264	  };
265	
266	  getSnapshot = (): GameSnapshot => this.snapshot;
267	
268	  /** Drains queued one-shot gameplay events for the shell (death screen, audio). */
269	  consumeEvents(): GameEvent[] {
270	    if (this.events.length === 0) return this.events;
271	    const drained = this.events;
272	    this.events = [];
273	    return drained;
274	  }
275	
276	  private emit = (event: GameEvent): void => {
277	    this.events.push(event);
278	  };
279	
280	  private applyDamage = (amount: number): void => {
281	    const heartsBefore = this.state.hearts;
282	    const died = applyDamageWithArmor(this.state, amount);
283	    this.syncEquippedArmor();
284	    if (died) {
285	      resetMining(this.state);
286	      this.emit({ type: "died" });
287	    } else if (this.state.hearts < heartsBefore) {
288	      this.emit({ type: "playerHurt" });
289	    }
290	  };
291	
292	  private removeMobAt = (index: number): void => {
293	    const state = this.state;
294	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
295	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
296	    const dropId = mob.hostile ? "cobble" : "food";
297	    state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, dropId, 1) ?? state.inventory;
298	  };
299	
300	  private get mobTickDeps() {
301	    return {
302	      surfaceYAt: this.surfaceYAt,
303	      applyDamage: this.applyDamage,
304	      removeMobAt: this.removeMobAt,
305	      rng: this.rng,
306	      emit: this.emit
307	    };
308	  }
309	
310	  private forceUnstuck(): void {
311	    const state = this.state;
312	    const safe = findSpawnOnLand(state.world, state.player.position.x, state.player.position.z, true);
313	    state.player.position.set(safe.x, safe.y, safe.z);
314	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
315	    state.player.onGround = false;
316	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
317	  }
318	
319	  private respawn(): void {
320	    const state = this.state;
321	    const spawn = randomLandPointNear(state.world, this.surfaceYAt, state.world.sizeX / 2, state.world.sizeZ / 2, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.9, this.rng);
322	    state.player.position.set(spawn.x, spawn.y + 2, spawn.z);
323	    state.player.velocity.set(0, 0, 0);
324	    state.player.pitch = 0;
325	    resetMining(state);
326	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
327	    this.events.push({ type: "respawned" });
328	  }
329	
330	  /** Unequips armor that left the inventory (broken or dropped). */
331	  private syncEquippedArmor(): void {
332	    const state = this.state;
333	    state.equippedArmor = inv.unequipMissingArmor(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor) ?? state.equippedArmor;
334	  }
335	
336	  /** Refreshes the F3 readout at ~4 Hz so React is not re-rendered every frame. */
337	  private tickDebugInfo(dt: number): void {
338	    const state = this.state;
339	    if (!state.debugOpen) return;
340	    state.timers.debugHudTimer += dt;
341	    if (state.timers.debugHudTimer < 0.25) return;
342	    state.timers.debugHudTimer = 0;
343	    state.debugInfo = this.currentDebugInfo();
344	  }
345	
346	  private currentDebugInfo() {
347	    const { player, daylight } = this.state;
348	    return {
349	      x: Math.round(player.position.x * 10) / 10,
350	      y: Math.round(player.position.y * 10) / 10,
351	      z: Math.round(player.position.z * 10) / 10,
352	      daylight: Math.round(daylight * 100) / 100
353	    };
354	  }
355	
356	  private buildSnapshot(): GameSnapshot {
357	    const state = this.state;
358	    return {
359	      api: this,
360	      inventory: state.inventory,
361	      equippedArmor: state.equippedArmor,
362	      selectedSlot: state.selectedSlot,
363	      hearts: state.hearts,
364	      hunger: state.hunger,
365	      daylightPercent: state.daylightPercent,
366	      passiveCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 0 : 1), 0),
367	      hostileCount: state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0),
368	      respawnSeconds: state.isDead ? Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.respawnTimer)) : 0,
369	      inventoryOpen: state.inventoryOpen,
370	      paused: state.paused,
371	      debugOpen: state.debugOpen,
372	      debug: state.debugInfo,
373	      armorPoints: inv.equippedDefense(state.inventory, state.equippedArmor),
374	      capsActive: state.capsActive
375	    };
376	  }
377	
378	  private refreshSnapshot(): void {
379	    const next = this.buildSnapshot();
380	    const prev = this.snapshot;
381	    const changed = (Object.keys(next) as Array<keyof GameSnapshot>).some((key) => next[key] !== prev[key]);
382	    if (!changed) return;
383	    this.snapshot = next;
384	    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
385	  }
386	}
387

1	import type { GameEngine } from "@/lib/game/engine/GameEngine";
2	import type { FrameInput } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
3	
4	const MOUSE_SENSITIVITY = 0.0021;
5	
6	type MutableInput = {
7	  keys: Set<string>;
8	  capsActive: boolean;
9	  leftMouseHeld: boolean;
10	  pointerLocked: boolean;
11	};
12	
13	export type InputController = {
14	  /** Live continuous-input view, passed to engine.step every frame. */
15	  readonly input: FrameInput;
16	  /** Drops held keys and the mouse button (on death/respawn). */
17	  clearKeys(): void;
18	  dispose(): void;
19	};
20	
21	type CreateInputControllerArgs = {
22	  /** The WebGL canvas — pointer lock target. */
23	  canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
24	  engine: GameEngine;
25	  onResize: () => void;
26	  onLockChange: (locked: boolean) => void;
27	};
28	
29	/**
30	 * Owns every DOM listener. Continuous input (keys, mouse button, pointer
31	 * lock) is exposed as a FrameInput the engine reads each step; discrete
32	 * actions become engine commands; mouse-look goes through engine.applyLook.
33	 */
34	export function createInputController(args: CreateInputControllerArgs): InputController {
35	  const { canvas, engine, onResize, onLockChange } = args;
36	
37	  const input: MutableInput = {
38	    keys: new Set<string>(),
39	    capsActive: false,
40	    leftMouseHeld: false,
41	    pointerLocked: false
42	  };
43	
44	  const uiBlocked = () => engine.state.inventoryOpen || engine.state.isDead || engine.state.paused;
45	
46	  const onMouseMove = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
47	    if (!input.pointerLocked) return;
48	    engine.applyLook(-evt.movementX * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY, -evt.movementY * MOUSE_SENSITIVITY);
49	  };
50	
51	  const onKeyDown = (evt: KeyboardEvent) => {
52	    // Escape under pointer lock never reaches us — the browser consumes it to
53	    // exit the lock, and the pointerlockchange handler below opens the menu.
54	    if (evt.code === "Escape") {
55	      if (engine.state.paused) engine.dispatch({ type: "resume" });
56	      else if (engine.state.inventoryOpen) engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleInventory" });
57	      else if (!input.pointerLocked) engine.dispatch({ type: "pause" });
58	      return;
59	    }
60	
61	    if (engine.state.paused) return;
62	
63	    if (evt.code === "F3") {
64	      evt.preventDefault();
65	      engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleDebug" });
66	      return;
67	    }
68	
69	    if (evt.code.startsWith("Digit")) {
70	      const idx = Number.parseInt(evt.code.slice(5), 10) - 1;
71	      if (idx >= 0) engine.dispatch({ type: "selectSlot", index: idx });
72	    }
73	
74	    if (evt.code === "KeyI") {
75	      engine.dispatch({ type: "toggleInventory" });
76	      input.keys.clear();
77	      if (input.pointerLocked) document.exitPointerLock();
78	      return;
79	    }
80	
81	    if (evt.code === "KeyU") {
82	      engine.dispatch({ type: "unstuck" });
83	      return;
84	    }
85	
86	    if (uiBlocked()) return;
87	
88	    if (evt.code === "KeyE") {
89	      evt.preventDefault();
90	      engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" });
91	    }
92	    if (evt.code === "KeyF") {
93	      evt.preventDefault();
94	      engine.dispatch({ type: "eatFood" });
95	    }
96	
97	    input.keys.add(evt.code);
98	    input.capsActive = evt.getModifierState("CapsLock");
99	    if (evt.code === "Space") evt.preventDefault();
100	  };
101	
102	  const onKeyUp = (evt: KeyboardEvent) => {
103	    input.keys.delete(evt.code);
104	    input.capsActive = evt.getModifierState("CapsLock");
105	  };
106	
107	  const onMouseDown = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
108	    if (uiBlocked()) return;
109	    if (!input.pointerLocked) {
110	      // The first click only acquires pointer lock — no mining or placing.
111	      // The request can legitimately reject (recent Esc, unfocused document,
112	      // headless browsers); the game just stays unlocked.
113	      Promise.resolve(canvas.requestPointerLock()).catch(() => {});
114	      return;
115	    }
116	
117	    if (evt.button === 0) {
118	      input.leftMouseHeld = true;
119	      engine.dispatch({ type: "attack" });
120	    }
121	    if (evt.button === 2) engine.dispatch({ type: "placeBlock" });
122	  };
123	
124	  const onMouseUp = (evt: MouseEvent) => {
125	    if (evt.button !== 0) return;
126	    input.leftMouseHeld = false;
127	  };
128	
129	  const onContextMenu = (evt: MouseEvent) => evt.preventDefault();
130	
131	  const onPointerLockChange = () => {
132	    input.pointerLocked = document.pointerLockElement === canvas;
133	    onLockChange(input.pointerLocked);
134	    // Losing the lock during plain gameplay means the player pressed Escape
135	    // (or the browser took it away) — open the pause menu. The inventory and
136	    // death paths set their state flags before the lock change fires, and the
137	    // pause command itself ignores those states as a second guard.
138	    if (!input.pointerLocked) engine.dispatch({ type: "pause" });
139	  };
140	
141	  window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);
142	  document.addEventListener("mousemove", onMouseMove);
143	  document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
144	  document.addEventListener("keyup", onKeyUp);
145	  document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown);
146	  document.addEventListener("mouseup", onMouseUp);
147	  document.addEventListener("contextmenu", onContextMenu);
148	  document.addEventListener("pointerlockchange", onPointerLockChange);
149	
150	  return {
151	    input,
152	
153	    clearKeys() {
154	      input.keys.clear();

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { HELD_BLOCK_COLORS, HELD_BLOCK_FALLBACK_COLOR } from "@/lib/world";
3	import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";
4	import { renderSpritePixels } from "@/lib/ui/spritePixels";
5	import { buildExtrudedSpriteGeometry } from "./extrudedSprite";
6	import { computeHeldPose } from "./heldItemPose";
7	
8	export type HeldItemFrame = {
9	  timeMs: number;
10	  /** True while the player is actively mining a block — loops the swing arc. */
11	  miningActive: boolean;
12	  /** 0..1 horizontal speed relative to walk speed — scales the walk bob. */
13	  moveFactor: number;
14	};
15	
16	export type HeldItemView = {
17	  /** Applies the animated pose every frame and rebuilds the model when the selected item changes. */
18	  update(slot: InventorySlot | undefined, frame: HeldItemFrame): void;
19	  /** Queues a one-shot swing (attack click); latched on the next update. */
20	  triggerSwing(): void;
21	  dispose(): void;
22	};
23	
24	export function createHeldItemView(camera: THREE.Camera): HeldItemView {
25	  const root = new THREE.Group();
26	  camera.add(root);
27	
28	  // The holder carries the animated pose; the model inside stays geometry-local.
29	  const holder = new THREE.Group();
30	  root.add(holder);
31	
32	  const geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[] = [];
33	  const materials: THREE.Material[] = [];
34	  let mesh: THREE.Object3D | null = null;
35	  let key = "";
36	
37	  let swingQueued = false;
38	  let swingStartMs = -Infinity;
39	  let equipStartMs = -Infinity;
40	  let wasMining = false;
41	
42	  const clear = () => {
43	    if (mesh) holder.remove(mesh);
44	    mesh = null;
45	    key = "";
46	    while (geometries.length) geometries.pop()?.dispose();
47	    while (materials.length) materials.pop()?.dispose();
48	  };
49	
50	  const blockColor = (blockId: number | undefined): number =>
51	    (blockId !== undefined ? HELD_BLOCK_COLORS[blockId as keyof typeof HELD_BLOCK_COLORS] : undefined) ?? HELD_BLOCK_FALLBACK_COLOR;
52	
53	  const buildModel = (slot: InventorySlot, id: string): THREE.Object3D => {
54	    if (slot.kind === "block") {
55	      const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.22, 0.22, 0.22);
56	      const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: blockColor(slot.blockId), roughness: 0.7, metalness: 0.05 });
57	      geometries.push(geometry);
58	      materials.push(material);
59	      return new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
60	    }
61	    // Tools, weapons, food, armor: extrude the same 16x16 pixel grid the
62	    // inventory icon uses, so the in-hand model always matches the sprite.
63	    const geometry = buildExtrudedSpriteGeometry(renderSpritePixels(id));
64	    const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.7, metalness: 0.05 });
65	    geometries.push(geometry);
66	    materials.push(material);
67	    return new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
68	  };
69	
70	  return {
71	    update(slot, frame) {
72	      const nextKey = slot?.id && slot.count > 0 && slot.kind ? `${slot.id}` : "";
73	      if (nextKey !== key) {
74	        clear();
75	        // The new item starts from its own equip transition — don't let it
76	        // inherit the previous item's swing phase.
77	        swingQueued = false;
78	        swingStartMs = -Infinity;
79	        wasMining = false;
80	        if (slot && nextKey) {
81	          mesh = buildModel(slot, nextKey);
82	          holder.add(mesh);
83	          key = nextKey;
84	          equipStartMs = frame.timeMs;
85	        }
86	      }
87	
88	      if (swingQueued) {
89	        swingQueued = false;
90	        swingStartMs = frame.timeMs;
91	      }
92	      if (frame.miningActive && !wasMining) swingStartMs = frame.timeMs;
93	      wasMining = frame.miningActive;
94	
95	      const pose = computeHeldPose({
96	        timeMs: frame.timeMs,
97	        swingStartMs,
98	        continuousSwing: frame.miningActive,
99	        equipStartMs,
100	        moveFactor: frame.moveFactor
101	      });
102	      holder.position.set(pose.posX, pose.posY, pose.posZ);
103	      holder.rotation.set(pose.rotX, pose.rotY, pose.rotZ);
104	    },
105	
106	    triggerSwing() {
107	      swingQueued = true;
108	    },
109	
110	    dispose() {
111	      clear();
112	      camera.remove(root);
113	    }
114	  };
115	}
116
