Material readback deliberately skipped (would need a debug getter for one assertion; console-error fixture + persistence is sufficient signal).\n- **Baseline before PR**: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`, `bun run test:e2e`.\n- **Manual pass** (visual change, per docs/testing.md): V into third person, Esc, click each preset — instant recolor behind the dimmed menu; portraits match the 3D palette; knight visor and robot eyes read at distance; reload keeps the choice with no default-color flash.\n\n## Branch & PR\n\nWork on `feature/player-skins`, the five slices above; after the baseline is green, push and open a PR against `hutusi/monecraft` `main` (no AI-attribution lines), noting: preference stored outside the world save, renderer kept id-agnostic, emissive robot eyes deferred to v2.\n"

## Approved Plan:
# Player Skin Presets (Appearance Picker)

## Context

The third-person player body (shipped in PR #9) has its palette burned in as seven module constants in `lib/game/render/playerModel.ts`. This feature lets players pick their character's look from six classic preset palettes — **Default** (current colors), **Alex** (pale skin, orange hair, green shirt), **Zombie**, **Skeleton**, **Knight** (steel tones, dark visor eye band), **Robot** (gunmetal, glowing cyan eyes) — from a new "Appearance" section in the pause menu, with a generated 16×16 pixel-art bust portrait per preset.

Key decisions (made with the user): all six presets ship; portraits are pixel busts via the existing zero-asset sprite system. Design constraints: skin choice is a **player preference, not world state** — own localStorage key (`minecraft_skin_v1`) mirroring the audio settings pattern, never engine state or save data; selection applies **live** via `material.color.setHex()` (no model rebuild); colors only in v1 (same geometry for all presets).

Branch: `feature/player-skins` off `main`.

## Design

### New: `lib/game/playerSkins.ts` — presets (pure)

```ts
export type PlayerPalette = { skin; hair; shirt; pants; shoes; eyeWhite; eyePupil: number };
export type SkinId = "default" | "alex" | "zombie" | "skeleton" | "knight" | "robot";
export type SkinPreset = { id: SkinId; label: string; palette: PlayerPalette };
export const DEFAULT_SKIN_ID: SkinId = "default";
export const DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE: PlayerPalette;  // alias of the default preset's palette
export const SKIN_PRESETS: readonly SkinPreset[];
export function isSkinId(value: unknown): value is SkinId;
export function getSkinPreset(id: string): SkinPreset;  // unknown id → default preset
```

Palettes (hex):

| preset | skin | hair | shirt | pants | shoes | eyeWhite | eyePupil |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Default | c68e63 | 4a3220 | 2e8b83 | 3b3f8f | 4a3527 | ffffff | 2b2b45 |
| Alex | e6b18a | b5602e | 5f8a3a | 6e5036 | 57422c | ffffff | 3f6b3a |
| Zombie | 6a9a4e | 4e7a3a | 2f6e62 | 4f4377 | 3d3326 | 1d2b1d | 0a120a |
| Skeleton | dcdcd2 | c4c4ba | b1b1a7 | 9a9a90 | 83837a | 2e2e2e | 121212 |
| Knight | aab2bd | 7c8591 | 8d96a3 | 646d79 | 4a525c | 161a20 | 161a20 |
| Robot | 767f8a | 4d545e | 5b636d | 444b54 | 32373e | 35e9ff | 0b6f80 |

Knight's eyeWhite == eyePupil intentionally reads as a continuous dark visor slit. Robot's bright cyan eyeWhite reads as a glow (true emissive is a deliberate v2 omission — keeps `setPalette` color-only).

### New: `lib/game/skinSettings.ts` — persistence (mirror `lib/game/audio/settings.ts`)

`SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY = "minecraft_skin_v1"`, `SkinSettings = { skinId: SkinId }` (object for forward-compat), `readSkinSettings(storage = localStorage)` with try/catch + `isSkinId` validation → default fallback, `writeSkinSettings` with silent quota-failure catch.

### Modified: `lib/game/render/playerModel.ts`

- Drop the 7 color constants; `createPlayerModel(palette: PlayerPalette = DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE)` — default param keeps all existing call sites/tests untouched.
- Extend `PlayerModel` with `paletteMaterials: Record<keyof PlayerPalette, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>` (same instances also stay in `materials`, which remains the sole disposal authority).
- New `export function applyPalette(model, palette)` — `color.setHex()` per key; allocates nothing.

### Modified: `lib/game/render/playerVisuals.ts` + `GameRenderer.ts`

- `PlayerVisuals.setPalette(palette)` → `applyPalette(model, palette)`.
- `GameRenderer.setPlayerSkin(palette)` next to `triggerSwing()` — renderer stays id-agnostic; the hook resolves id → palette.
- No constructor param for the initial palette: the renderer effect calls `setPlayerSkin` synchronously after `GameRenderer.create`, before the first rAF, and the body boots invisible (first-person), so no default-palette flash is possible.

### Modified: `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts` (mirror the audio pattern, lines ~74-96)

- `const [skinId, setSkinId] = useState<SkinId>(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID)` + `skinIdRef` + new `rendererRef = useRef<GameRenderer | null>(null)`.
- Extend the existing post-mount storage effect (the one calling `readAudioSettings`): read `readSkinSettings()`, set ref, `queueMicrotask(() => setSkinId(...))`. Ordering is safe: the renderer effect runs in a later commit (ctx set via the `attachMount` callback ref), the same guarantee audio already relies on.
- In the renderer effect after `canvasRef.current = renderer.domElement`: set `rendererRef.current` and call `renderer.setPlayerSkin(getSkinPreset(skinIdRef.current).palette)`; null the ref in cleanup.
- `updateSkin(id)` callback: set ref + state, `writeSkinSettings({ skinId: id })`, `rendererRef.current?.setPlayerSkin(...)`. Return `skinId, updateSkin`.

### New: `lib/ui/skinPortrait.ts` — bust portrait

`renderSkinPortraitPixels(palette): Uint8ClampedArray` — 16×16 pixel bust (hair rows, skin face with eyeWhite/pupil pixels, neck with shadow shades via a `shade(rgb, 0.75)` helper, shirt shoulders) using `paintGrid` from `lib/ui/spritePixels.ts`. Add to `lib/ui/sprites.ts`:
`skinPortraitUrl(skinId) = spriteDataUrl(\`skin:${skinId}\`, () => renderSkinPortraitPixels(getSkinPreset(skinId).palette))` — reuses the cache + happy-dom transparent degradation (`lib/ui` already imports from `lib/game`, so the dependency direction is established).

### Modified: `components/game/PauseMenu.tsx` + `MinecraftGame.tsx` + `app/hud.css`

- PauseMenu props: `skinId: SkinId; onSkinChange: (id: SkinId) => void`. New `.pause-skins` section between `.pause-sound` and the save message: "Appearance" title + 3-column grid of six `.skin-swatch` buttons, each `aria-pressed={preset.id === skinId}`, `aria-label="${label} skin"`, containing a `PixelImg` portrait (decorative, `aria-hidden`) + label text.
- `MinecraftGame.tsx`: pass `skinId`/`onSkinChange` from the hook into `<PauseMenu>`.
- hud.css: `.pause-skins`, `.pause-skins-grid` (3 columns), `.skin-swatch` reusing the `.mc-button` bevel recipe (copy the exact border-color values), `[aria-pressed="true"]` gold-bevel highlight — selected state driven off the accessible attribute.

## Commit slices (lint + tests green at each)

1. **Add player skin presets and persisted skin selection** — `playerSkins.ts`, `skinSettings.ts` + tests (6 unique presets; `getSkinPreset("nope")` → default; storage roundtrip / missing / corrupt JSON / unknown id / throwing setItem, Map-backed fake Storage copied from `lib/game/audio/settings.test.ts`).
2. **Parameterize the player body palette and recolor it live** — `playerModel.ts`, `playerVisuals.ts`, `GameRenderer.ts` + test extensions (palette lands on named materials; `applyPalette` mutates the same material instances, `materials.length` unchanged; visuals `setPalette` visible through the scene). Existing model tests pass via the default param.
3. **Generate 16x16 bust portraits for skin presets** — `lib/ui/skinPortrait.ts` + test (every preset → 16×16×4 buffer, >40 opaque pixels, deterministic, six pairwise distinct, hair/shirt rows use palette colors), `sprites.ts` `skinPortraitUrl`.
4. **Add an Appearance picker to the pause menu** — `PauseMenu.tsx` + tests (six buttons by role/name `/skin$/`, `aria-pressed` only on selected, click fires `onSkinChange` with the id; NO image-pixel assertions — happy-dom), `MinecraftGame.tsx`, `useMinecraftGame.ts`, `hud.css`, e2e addition.
5. **Document the skin presets** — README features/controls note, CHANGELOG entry ("session preference under `minecraft_skin_v1`; no save-format or worldgen impact"), `docs/adding-content.md` "Adding a skin preset" note, docs/testing.md + docs/architecture.md rows.

## Verification

- **Unit**: per-slice above (`bun test`).
- **E2E** (`e2e/smoke.e2e.ts`): Escape to pause → click "Robot skin" → `JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("minecraft_skin_v1"))` equals `{ skinId: "robot" }` → reload (existing boot-wait helper) → Escape → the Robot button has `aria-pressed="true"`. Material readback deliberately skipped (would need a debug getter for one assertion; console-error fixture + persistence is sufficient signal).
- **Baseline before PR**: `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`, `bun run test:e2e`.
- **Manual pass** (visual change, per docs/testing.md): V into third person, Esc, click each preset — instant recolor behind the dimmed menu; portraits match the 3D palette; knight visor and robot eyes read at distance; reload keeps the choice with no default-color flash.

## Branch & PR

Work on `feature/player-skins`, the five slices above; after the baseline is green, push and open a PR against `hutusi/monecraft` `main` (no AI-attribution lines), noting: preference stored outside the world save, renderer kept id-agnostic, emissive robot eyes deferred to v2.

```

```
1	import { DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS, type AudioSettings } from "./audioDirector";
2	
3	/** Own localStorage key — volume preferences are not part of the world save. */
4	export const AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY = "minecraft_audio_v1";
5	
6	function clamp01(value: unknown, fallback: number): number {
7	  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) return fallback;
8	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value));
9	}
10	
11	// Storage is injectable so settings logic can be tested without a browser.
12	export function readAudioSettings(storage: Storage = localStorage): AudioSettings {
13	  try {
14	    const raw = storage.getItem(AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY);
15	    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS;
16	    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<AudioSettings> | null;
17	    return {
18	      master: clamp01(parsed?.master, DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS.master),
19	      music: clamp01(parsed?.music, DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS.music),
20	      muted: parsed?.muted === true
21	    };
22	  } catch {
23	    return DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS;
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	export function writeAudioSettings(settings: AudioSettings, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {
28	  try {
29	    storage.setItem(AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));
30	  } catch {
31	    // Quota/privacy-mode failures just lose the preference, never the game.
32	  }
33	}
34	
```

1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS } from "./audioDirector";
3	import { AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY, readAudioSettings, writeAudioSettings } from "./settings";
4	
5	function fakeStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {
6	  const map = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
7	  return {
8	    get length() {
9	      return map.size;
10	    },
11	    clear: () => map.clear(),
12	    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,
13	    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,
14	    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),
15	    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void map.set(key, value)
16	  };
17	}
18	
19	describe("audio settings persistence", () => {
20	  test("round-trips through storage", () => {
21	    const storage = fakeStorage();
22	    writeAudioSettings({ master: 0.4, music: 0.1, muted: true }, storage);
23	    expect(readAudioSettings(storage)).toEqual({ master: 0.4, music: 0.1, muted: true });
24	  });
25	
26	  test("missing or corrupt data falls back to defaults", () => {
27	    expect(readAudioSettings(fakeStorage())).toEqual(DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS);
28	    expect(readAudioSettings(fakeStorage({ [AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY]: "not json{" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS);
29	    expect(readAudioSettings(fakeStorage({ [AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY]: "null" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS);
30	  });
31	
32	  test("out-of-range and wrong-typed fields are sanitized", () => {
33	    const storage = fakeStorage({
34	      [AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ master: 7, music: -2, muted: "yes" })
35	    });
36	    expect(readAudioSettings(storage)).toEqual({ master: 1, music: 0, muted: false });
37	  });
38	});
39

palette the body model boots with before any preference is applied. */\nexport const DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE: PlayerPalette = SKIN_PRESETS[0].palette;\n\nexport function isSkinId(value: unknown): value is SkinId {\n  return typeof value === \"string\" && SKIN_PRESETS.some((preset) => preset.id === value);\n}\n\n/** Resolves an id from storage/UI; unknown or corrupt ids fall back to the default preset. */\nexport function getSkinPreset(id: string): SkinPreset {\n  return SKIN_PRESETS.find((preset) => preset.id === id) ?? SKIN_PRESETS[0];\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/skinSettings.ts",
  "content": "import { DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, isSkinId, type SkinId } from \"./playerSkins\";\n\n/** Own localStorage key — the skin choice is a player preference, not part of the world save. */\nexport const SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY = \"minecraft_skin_v1\";\n\n/** An object (not a bare id) so future appearance fields don't need a key migration. */\nexport type SkinSettings = {\n  skinId: SkinId;\n};\n\nexport const DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS: SkinSettings = { skinId: DEFAULT_SKIN_ID };\n\n// Storage is injectable so settings logic can be tested without a browser.\nexport function readSkinSettings(storage: Storage = localStorage): SkinSettings {\n  try {\n    const raw = storage.getItem(SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY);\n    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS;\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<SkinSettings> | null;\n    return { skinId: isSkinId(parsed?.skinId) ? parsed.skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID };\n  } catch {\n    return DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function writeSkinSettings(settings: SkinSettings, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {\n  try {\n    storage.setItem(SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY, JSON.stringify(settings));\n  } catch {\n    // Quota/privacy-mode failures just lose the preference, never the game.\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE, DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, getSkinPreset, isSkinId, SKIN_PRESETS } from \"./playerSkins\";\n\ndescribe(\"skin presets\", () => {\n  test(\"ships six presets with unique ids and labels\", () => {\n    expect(SKIN_PRESETS).toHaveLength(6);\n    expect(new Set(SKIN_PRESETS.map((preset) => preset.id)).size).toBe(6);\n    expect(new Set(SKIN_PRESETS.map((preset) => preset.label)).size).toBe(6);\n  });\n\n  test(\"every palette field is a valid 24-bit color\", () => {\n    for (const preset of SKIN_PRESETS) {\n      for (const value of Object.values(preset.palette)) {\n        expect(Number.isInteger(value)).toBe(true);\n        expect(value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n        expect(value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0xffffff);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n\n  test(\"the default preset is first and backs DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE\", () => {\n    expect(SKIN_PRESETS[0].id).toBe(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID);\n    expect(DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE).toBe(SKIN_PRESETS[0].palette);\n  });\n\n  test(\"getSkinPreset resolves known ids and falls back to default\", () => {\n    expect(getSkinPreset(\"robot\").id).toBe(\"robot\");\n    expect(getSkinPreset(\"nope\").id).toBe(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID);\n    expect(getSkinPreset(\"\").id).toBe(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID);\n  });\n\n  test(\"isSkinId guards arbitrary values\", () => {\n    expect(isSkinId(\"knight\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(isSkinId(\"herobrine\")).toBe(false);\n    expect(isSkinId(42)).toBe(false);\n    expect(isSkinId(undefined)).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/skinSettings.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS, readSkinSettings, SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY, writeSkinSettings } from \"./skinSettings\";\n\nfunction fakeStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {\n  const map = new Map(Object.entries(initial));\n  return {\n    get length() {\n      return map.size;\n    },\n    clear: () => map.clear(),\n    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,\n    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),\n    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void map.set(key, value)\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"skin settings persistence\", () => {\n  test(\"round-trips through storage\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    writeSkinSettings({ skinId: \"robot\" }, storage);\n    expect(readSkinSettings(storage)).toEqual({ skinId: \"robot\" });\n  });\n\n  test(\"missing or corrupt data falls back to the default skin\", () => {\n    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage())).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);\n    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: \"not json{\" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);\n    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: \"null\" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);\n  });\n\n  test(\"unknown or wrong-typed skin ids are sanitized to the default\", () => {\n    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ skinId: \"herobrine\" }) }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);\n    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ skinId: 7 }) }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);\n  });\n\n  test(\"write failures are swallowed\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    storage.setItem = () => {\n      throw new Error(\"quota\");\n    };\n    expect(() => writeSkinSettings({ skinId: \"alex\" }, storage)).not.toThrow();\n  });\n});\n"
}

  "new_string": "  /** Hand anchor inside the right arm — the held item model goes here. */\n  itemHolder: THREE.Group;\n  /** One material per palette slot, for live recolors; also listed in `materials`. */\n  paletteMaterials: Record<keyof PlayerPalette, THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>;\n  materials: THREE.Material[];\n  geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[];\n};\n\n/** Recolors an existing model in place — no allocation, safe to call live. */\nexport function applyPalette(model: PlayerModel, palette: PlayerPalette): void {\n  for (const key of Object.keys(model.paletteMaterials) as Array<keyof PlayerPalette>) {\n    model.paletteMaterials[key].color.setHex(palette[key]);\n  }\n}\n\nexport function createPlayerModel(palette: PlayerPalette = DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE): PlayerModel {\n  const group = new THREE.Group();\n  const materials: THREE.Material[] = [];\n  const geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[] = [];\n\n  const material = (color: number) => {\n    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.02 });\n    materials.push(mat);\n    return mat;\n  };\n  const box = (w: number, h: number, d: number, mat: THREE.Material) => {\n    const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);\n    geometries.push(geometry);\n    return new THREE.Mesh(geometry, mat);\n  };\n\n  // `materials` stays the single disposal authority; this record only names them.\n  const paletteMaterials: PlayerModel[\"paletteMaterials\"] = {\n    skin: material(palette.skin),\n    hair: material(palette.hair),\n    shirt: material(palette.shirt),\n    pants: material(palette.pants),\n    shoes: material(palette.shoes),\n    eyeWhite: material(palette.eyeWhite),\n    eyePupil: material(palette.eyePupil)\n  };\n  const skinMat = paletteMaterials.skin;\n  const shirtMat = paletteMaterials.shirt;\n  const pantsMat = paletteMaterials.pants;\n  const shoeMat = paletteMaterials.shoes;"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/ui/skinPortrait.ts",
  "content": "import type { PlayerPalette } from \"@/lib/game/playerSkins\";\nimport { paintGrid, type Rgb } from \"./spritePixels\";\n\n/**\n * 16x16 pixel bust (head + shoulders) for the pause-menu skin picker, derived\n * from the same palette that colors the 3D body — so the swatch is\n * automatically truthful for any preset. Legend: h hair, s skin, S skin\n * shadow, w eye white, p pupil, t shirt, T shirt shadow.\n */\nconst BUST_GRID = [\n  \"................\",\n  \"...hhhhhhhhhh...\",\n  \"...hhhhhhhhhh...\",\n  \"...hhhhhhhhhh...\",\n  \"...hssssssssh...\",\n  \"...ssssssssss...\",\n  \"...sswwsswwss...\",\n  \"...sswpsspwss...\",\n  \"...ssssssssss...\",\n  \"...sssSSSSsss...\",\n  \"....SssssssS....\",\n  \"......ssss......\",\n  \"......SssS......\",\n  \".tttttttttttttt.\",\n  \"tttttttttttttttt\",\n  \"TTTTTTTTTTTTTTTT\"\n];\n\nconst toRgb = (hex: number): Rgb => [(hex >> 16) & 255, (hex >> 8) & 255, hex & 255];\n\nconst shade = (rgb: Rgb, factor: number): Rgb => [Math.round(rgb[0] * factor), Math.round(rgb[1] * factor), Math.round(rgb[2] * factor)];\n\nexport function renderSkinPortraitPixels(palette: PlayerPalette): Uint8ClampedArray {\n  const skin = toRgb(palette.skin);\n  const shirt = toRgb(palette.shirt);\n  return paintGrid(BUST_GRID, {\n    h: toRgb(palette.hair),\n    s: skin,\n    S: shade(skin, 0.75),\n    w: toRgb(palette.eyeWhite),\n    p: toRgb(palette.eyePupil),\n    t: shirt,\n    T: shade(shirt, 0.75)\n  });\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/ui/skinPortrait.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { renderSkinPortraitPixels } from \"./skinPortrait\";\nimport { SKIN_PRESETS } from \"@/lib/game/playerSkins\";\nimport { SPRITE_SIZE } from \"./spritePixels\";\n\nfunction opaqueCount(pixels: Uint8ClampedArray): number {\n  let count = 0;\n  for (let i = 3; i < pixels.length; i += 4) {\n    if (pixels[i] === 255) count += 1;\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\nfunction pixelAt(pixels: Uint8ClampedArray, x: number, y: number): [number, number, number] {\n  const i = (y * SPRITE_SIZE + x) * 4;\n  return [pixels[i], pixels[i + 1], pixels[i + 2]];\n}\n\ndescribe(\"skin portraits\", () => {\n  test(\"every preset renders a substantial deterministic 16x16 bust\", () => {\n    for (const preset of SKIN_PRESETS) {\n      const pixels = renderSkinPortraitPixels(preset.palette);\n      expect(pixels.length).toBe(SPRITE_SIZE * SPRITE_SIZE * 4);\n      expect(opaqueCount(pixels)).toBeGreaterThan(40);\n      expect(renderSkinPortraitPixels(preset.palette)).toEqual(pixels);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test(\"all six portraits are pairwise distinct\", () => {\n    const rendered = SKIN_PRESETS.map((preset) => renderSkinPortraitPixels(preset.palette).join(\",\"));\n    expect(new Set(rendered).size).toBe(SKIN_PRESETS.length);\n  });\n\n  test(\"hair, eye, and shirt rows use the preset's palette colors\", () => {\n    const preset = SKIN_PRESETS.find((entry) => entry.id === \"alex\")!;\n    const { hair, shirt, eyeWhite } = preset.palette;\n    const pixels = renderSkinPortraitPixels(preset.palette);\n    expect(pixelAt(pixels, 8, 2)).toEqual([(hair >> 16) & 255, (hair >> 8) & 255, hair & 255]);\n    expect(pixelAt(pixels, 6, 6)).toEqual([(eyeWhite >> 16) & 255, (eyeWhite >> 8) & 255, eyeWhite & 255]);\n    expect(pixelAt(pixels, 8, 14)).toEqual([(shirt >> 16) & 255, (shirt >> 8) & 255, shirt & 255]);\n  });\n});\n"
}

9	  onSave: () => void;

16	  ["Sprint / Crouch", "W + CapsLock / C"],
17	  ["Mine / Attack", "Hold left click / Left click"],
18	  ["Place block", "Right click or E"],
19	  ["Inventory", "I"],
20	  ["Eat food", "F"],

27	/**
28	 * The Minecraft-style game menu: shown while the engine is paused, with the
29	 * classic column of gray beveled buttons over a dimmed (frozen) world.
30	 */
31	export default function PauseMenu({ saveMessage, audioSettings, onAudioSettingsChange, onBack, onSave, onLoad, onReset }: PauseMenuProps) {
32	  const [confirmingReset, setConfirmingReset] = useState(false);
33	
34	  const volumeSlider = (label: string, value: number, onValue: (volume: number) => void) => (
35	    <label className="pause-sound-row">
36	      <span>{label}</span>
37	      <input
38	        className="mc-slider"
39	        type="range"
40	        min={0}
41	        max={100}
42	        value={Math.round(value * 100)}
43	        onChange={(event) => onValue(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(event.target.value) / 100)))}
44	        aria-label={`${label} volume`}
45	      />
46	    </label>
47	  );
48	
49	  return (
50	    <div className="pause-overlay">
51	      <div className="pause-menu">
52	        <div className="pause-title">Game Menu</div>
53	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onBack}>
54	          Back to Game
55	        </button>
56	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onSave}>
57	          Save Game
58	        </button>
59	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onLoad}>
60	          Load Save
61	        </button>
62	        {confirmingReset ? (
63	          <div className="pause-reset-row">
64	            <button className="mc-button danger" onClick={onReset}>
65	              Confirm Reset
66	            </button>
67	            <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingReset(false)}>
68	              Cancel
69	            </button>
70	          </div>
71	        ) : (
72	          <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingReset(true)}>
73	            Reset World
74	          </button>
75	        )}

31:export default function PixelImg({ src, alt, size, className, "aria-hidden": ariaHidden }: PixelImgProps) {
/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element -- tiny generated data-URL sprites; next/image cannot optimize them */

import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { TRANSPARENT_PIXEL } from "@/lib/ui/sprites";

type PixelImgProps = {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  size: number;
  className?: string;
  "aria-hidden"?: boolean;
};

const noopSubscribe = () => () => {};

/**
 * True after hydration. Sprites are canvas-generated, so the server can only
 * ever render the transparent fallback; the hydration render must match it
 * (React does not patch mismatched src attributes), and the real sprite swaps
 * in on the immediate follow-up render.
 */
function useHydrated(): boolean {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    noopSubscribe,
    () => true,
    () => false
  );
}

/** An <img> for generated pixel-art sprites: never draggable, always pixelated. */

127	    const { engine: gameEngine, node } = ctx;

130	    if (!created.ok) {
131	      // Microtask: reporting an init failure from inside the effect body
132	      // would count as a cascading synchronous setState.
133	      queueMicrotask(() => setRendererError(created.error));
134	      return;
135	    }
136	    const renderer = created.renderer;
137	    canvasRef.current = renderer.domElement;

139	    const audio = createAudioDirector();
140	    audio.setSettings(audioSettingsRef.current);
141	    audioRef.current = audio;
142	    const input = createInputController({
143	      canvas: renderer.domElement,
144	      engine: gameEngine,
145	      onResize: () => renderer.handleResize(),
146	      onLockChange: (isLocked) => {
147	        // Pointer-lock acquisition is itself a user gesture — a safe unlock
148	        // point, and it re-resumes a context suspended by the browser.
149	        if (isLocked) audio.unlock();
150	        setLocked(isLocked);
151	      }
152	    });
153	
154	    // Autoplay policy: the AudioContext may only start inside a user gesture.
155	    const unlockAudio = () => audio.unlock();
156	    document.addEventListener("mousedown", unlockAudio);
157	    document.addEventListener("keydown", unlockAudio);
158	
159	    const autoSave = () => persistGame(gameEngine, flashMessage);
160	    const autoSaveId = window.setInterval(autoSave, AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL_MS);
161	    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", autoSave);
162	
163	    window.__monecraft = { engine: gameEngine, renderer, input, audio };
164	
165	    let minimap: MinimapRenderer | null = null;
166	    let last = performance.now();
167	    let animationFrame = 0;
168	    // Catch-up stepping: a slow frame (software GL, busy machine) can take far
169	    // longer than one 50ms step, and a single clamped step would run the
170	    // simulation in slow motion. Bounded substeps keep sim time tracking wall
171	    // time; the cap bounds work per frame and quietly drops time beyond it
172	    // (e.g. after a background-tab stall).
173	    const MAX_STEP_SECONDS = 0.05;
174	    const MAX_SUBSTEPS = 5;
175	    let pendingSeconds = 0;
176	    const clock = () => {
177	      const now = performance.now();
178	      pendingSeconds = Math.min(pendingSeconds + (now - last) / 1000, MAX_STEP_SECONDS * MAX_SUBSTEPS);
179	      last = now;
180	
181	      let frameSeconds = 0;
182	      while (pendingSeconds > 0) {
183	        const dt = Math.min(pendingSeconds, MAX_STEP_SECONDS);
184	        gameEngine.step(dt, input.input);
185	        pendingSeconds -= dt;
186	        frameSeconds += dt;
187	      }
188	
189	      for (const event of gameEngine.consumeEvents()) {
190	        if (event.type === "died") {
191	          input.clearKeys();
192	          if (document.pointerLockElement === renderer.domElement) document.exitPointerLock();
193	        }
194	        if (event.type === "respawned") input.clearKeys();
195	        if (event.type === "attackSwung") renderer.triggerSwing();
196	        audio.handleEvent(event);
197	      }
198	
199	      if (!minimap && minimapNodeRef.current) minimap = createMinimapRenderer(minimapNodeRef.current);
200	      // The minimap must read worldMeshDirty before renderer.sync clears it.
201	      minimap?.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
202	      renderer.sync(gameEngine.state, now);
203	      audio.sync(gameEngine.state, frameSeconds);
204	      renderer.render();
205	      animationFrame = requestAnimationFrame(clock);

102	          saveMessage={saveMessage}
103	          audioSettings={audioSettings}
104	          onAudioSettingsChange={updateAudioSettings}
105	          onBack={resumeNow}
106	          onSave={saveNow}
107	          onLoad={loadNow}
108	          onReset={resetNow}
109	        />
110	      ) : null}
111	
112	      <DeathScreen seconds={respawnSeconds} onRespawn={respawnNow} />
113	
```

1	import { describe, expect, mock, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
3	import userEvent from "@testing-library/user-event";
4	import PauseMenu from "@/components/game/PauseMenu";
5	import { DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS } from "@/lib/game/audio/audioDirector";
6	
7	function renderMenu(overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof PauseMenu>[0]> = {}) {
8	  const props = {
9	    saveMessage: "",
10	    audioSettings: DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS,
11	    onAudioSettingsChange: mock(),
12	    onBack: mock(),
13	    onSave: mock(),
14	    onLoad: mock(),
15	    onReset: mock(),
16	    ...overrides
17	  };
18	  render(<PauseMenu {...props} />);
19	  return props;
20	}
21	
22	describe("PauseMenu", () => {
23	  test("buttons dispatch their callbacks", async () => {
24	    const user = userEvent.setup();
25	    const props = renderMenu();
26	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Back to Game" }));
27	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Save Game" }));
28	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Load Save" }));
29	    expect(props.onBack).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
30	    expect(props.onSave).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
31	    expect(props.onLoad).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
32	  });
33	
34	  test("reset requires a confirmation step", async () => {
35	    const user = userEvent.setup();
36	    const props = renderMenu();
37	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reset World" }));
38	    expect(props.onReset).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
39	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Confirm Reset" }));
40	    expect(props.onReset).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
41	  });
42	
43	  test("cancel backs out of the reset confirmation", async () => {
44	    const user = userEvent.setup();
45	    const props = renderMenu();
46	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reset World" }));
47	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Cancel" }));
48	    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Reset World" })).toBeTruthy();
49	    expect(props.onReset).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
50	  });
51	
52	  test("shows the transient save message", () => {
53	    renderMenu({ saveMessage: "Saved" });
54	    expect(screen.getByText("Saved")).toBeTruthy();
55	  });
56	
57	  test("volume sliders report normalized values", () => {
58	    const props = renderMenu({ audioSettings: { master: 0.8, music: 0.6, muted: false } });
59	    const master = screen.getByLabelText("Sound volume") as HTMLInputElement;
60	    expect(master.value).toBe("80");
61	    fireEvent.change(master, { target: { value: "35" } });
62	    expect(props.onAudioSettingsChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ master: 0.35 });
63	    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("Music volume"), { target: { value: "0" } });
64	    expect(props.onAudioSettingsChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ music: 0 });
65	  });
66	
67	  test("the mute button toggles by current state", async () => {
68	    const user = userEvent.setup();
69	    const props = renderMenu({ audioSettings: { master: 1, music: 1, muted: true } });
70	    await user.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "Unmute Sound" }));
71	    expect(props.onAudioSettingsChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ muted: false });
72	  });
73	});

```
## A new mob

- Add a template to `MOB_TEMPLATES` in `lib/game/mobs.ts` — `detectRange: 0` means passive (wanders, flees the player), `> 0` means hostile (chases, attacks with line-of-sight check).
- The model is assembled from `createMobModel(...)` color/size args in `lib/game/mobModel.ts` (legs animate automatically in `lib/game/render/mobVisuals.ts`).
- Wire spawning in `lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts`: `spawnInitialMobs` for the day-one population, `tickHostileSpawnDirector` for the night respawn loop.
- Give it a voice: `MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS` and `MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS` rows in `lib/game/audio/soundParams.ts`, and a call interval in `lib/game/audio/mobAmbience.ts` (`CALL_INTERVALS`) — all keyed by `MobKind`, so typecheck enforces them.
- A headless test in `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts` is cheap: boot the engine, fast-forward to night, assert the mob appears/behaves.

## A new sound

- One-shots ride on engine events: add a variant to `GameEvent` (`lib/game/engine/state.ts`), emit it from the relevant system via the injected `emit` callback, and route it in `audioDirector.ts`'s `handleEvent`. Continuous sounds derive from state in the director's `sync` instead.
- Design the sound by ear in the [ZZFX designer](https://killedbyapixel.github.io/ZzFX/), then transcribe the parameters into a `SoundDef` in `lib/game/audio/soundParams.ts` using the named-field `zz({...})` helper (field order matches the designer's positional array).
- Coverage is pinned: `materials.test.ts` and `soundParams.test.ts` fail on missing material/mob rows, and routing is testable headlessly with the fake `SynthBackend` pattern in `audioDirector.test.ts`. Actual sound quality is a manual `bun run dev` pass.
```

> AGENT

Done — **https://github.com/hutusi/monecraft/pull/10** is open with the full skin feature.

## What shipped

Five commits on `feature/player-skins`:

1. **Add player skin presets and persisted skin selection** — six palettes (Steve, Alex, Zombie, Skeleton, Knight, Robot) in `lib/game/playerSkins.ts`, persistence under `minecraft_skin_v1` with corrupt-data fallback
2. **Parameterize the player body palette and recolor it live** — `createPlayerModel(palette)` with named materials; a skin swap is just `color.setHex()` calls
3. **Generate 16×16 bust portraits** — pixel busts derived from each preset's palette via the existing sprite system, so swatches are automatically truthful
4. **Add an Appearance picker to the pause menu** — portrait-button grid, selection applies live through the renderer and survives world resets
5. **Document the skin presets** — README, CHANGELOG, an "adding a preset" recipe in `docs/adding-content.md`, architecture/testing docs

**Verification:** lint, typecheck, format, 263 unit tests (18 new), production build, and all 8 e2e tests green — including a new browser test that picks Robot, checks the localStorage write, reloads, and confirms the selection stuck.

Two notes:

- I rebased the branch onto `origin/main` before pushing so the PR doesn't include your local-only Entire commit — that one (`9723e08`) is still sitting unpushed on your local `main` whenever you want to push it.
- The palettes were designed in hex, not by eye. Worth a quick `bun run dev` pass (V → Esc → Appearance) before merging — especially whether the Knight visor and Robot's cyan eyes read well at distance. Each palette is one line in `lib/game/playerSkins.ts` if you want to tweak.

> AGENT

Here's the full status of PR #11:

**CI: `verify` failed, `e2e` passed — root cause was the Entire commit, now fixed and pushed.** The unformatted `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/agents/entire-search.md` you pushed to `origin/main` failed `format:check`, and since this branch sits on top of that commit, the PR's CI went red. I pushed `9677f6d` ("Format Entire config files") to the branch — CI is re-running now and the fix carries to `main` when the PR merges. I also reset my local `main` to match yours so the duplicate amended commit I'd made is gone.

**CodeRabbit: one Minor finding, fix committed locally — not pushed yet.** It flagged that `readSkinSettings` returns the shared `DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS` instance on its fallback paths, so a caller mutating the result could pollute later reads. Valid, if theoretical (no current caller mutates it). The fix (`6730d7c`, spread into a fresh object) is committed on the branch with tests green, waiting for your look before I push it — say the word and I'll push.

One observation for later: the audio settings module has the identical pattern (`readAudioSettings` returns `DEFAULT_AUDIO_SETTINGS` directly), which CodeRabbit didn't flag. If you care about consistency, that's a one-line follow-up — I left it alone to keep this PR scoped.

> AGENT

Merged ✅ — all checks were green (`verify`, `e2e`, and CodeRabbit's completed review), and PR #11 went into `main` as merge commit `b203001`. Local `main` is updated and the merged feature branch is deleted.

The skin feature is now live: six presets (Steve, Alex, Zombie, Skeleton, Knight, Robot) in the pause menu's Appearance section, with generated pixel portraits, live recoloring, and the choice persisting under `minecraft_skin_v1`. The Entire config formatting fix also rode in, so `main`'s CI is healthy again.

If any palette doesn't read well in-game, each one is a single line in `lib/game/playerSkins.ts` — and adding a seventh preset is the same one-line change (documented in `docs/adding-content.md`).
